let pools = {};

export const getAuthenticatedPool = async (identity) => {
  if (pools[identity.pk]) return pools[identity.pk];

  const { SimplePool, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
  const pool = new SimplePool({
    enablePing: false,
    enableReconnect: true,
    onauth: async (challenge) => {
      console.log("AUTH challenge:", challenge);
      return finalizeEvent({
        kind: 22242,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [
          ["relay", "wss://groups.aegispayhq.com"],
          ["challenge", challenge]
        ],
        content: ""
      }, identity.sk);
    }
  });

  const originalEnsure = pool.ensureRelay.bind(pool);
  pool.ensureRelay = async (url) => {
    const relay = await originalEnsure(url);
    if (url.includes("aegispayhq.com") && !relay.__patchedAuth) {
      relay.__patchedAuth = true;
      const originalSend = relay.send.bind(relay);
      relay.send = (msg) => {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(msg);
          if (parsed[0] === "AUTH" && typeof parsed[1] === "object") {
            msg = JSON.stringify(["AUTH", JSON.stringify(parsed[1])]);
          }
        } catch (e) {}
        originalSend(msg);
      };
    }
    return relay;
  };

  pools[identity.pk] = pool;
  return pool;
};

export const clearPool = () => {
  pools = {};
};

    window.navigator.vibrate(ms);
  }
};

// Strips EXIF/metadata from images by re-rendering them through a Canvas.
// Audio metadata is harder without external libs, so we focus on images first.

export const stripImageMetadata = async (file) => {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = img.width;
        canvas.height = img.height;
        
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
        
        // Export as a new Blob (this drops all original EXIF data)
        canvas.toBlob((blob) => {
          if (!blob) return reject(new Error('Canvas toBlob failed'));
          const cleanFile = new File([blob], file.name.split('.')[0] + '_clean.jpg', {
            type: 'image/jpeg',
            lastModified: Date.now()
          });
          resolve(cleanFile);
        }, 'image/jpeg', 0.9); // 90% quality
      };
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to load image for stripping'));
      img.src = e.target.result;
    };
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read file'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
};

=========================================
FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupCipher.js
=========================================

const subtle = globalThis.crypto.subtle;
const enc = new TextEncoder();
const dec = new TextDecoder();
export const CIPHER_VERSION = 1;
export function generateGroupKey() { const k = new Uint8Array(32); crypto.getRandomValues(k); return k; }
export function toHex(bytes) { return Array.from(bytes, b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join(''); }
export function fromHex(hex) { if (hex.length % 2) throw new Error('invalid hex'); const out = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2); for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) out[i] = parseInt(hex.slice(i * 2, i * 2 + 2), 16); return out; }
export function b64(bytes) { let s = ''; for (const b of bytes) s += String.fromCharCode(b); return btoa(s); }
export function unb64(s) { const bin = atob(s); const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i); return out; }
function aad({ groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) { return enc.encode(`cipher-group-v${CIPHER_VERSION}|${groupId}|${epoch}|${senderPubkey}`); }
async function importKey(raw) { return subtle.importKey('raw', raw, { name: 'AES-GCM' }, false, ['encrypt', 'decrypt']); }
export async function encryptGroupMessage({ rawKey, plaintext, groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) {
  const key = await importKey(rawKey);
  const iv = new Uint8Array(12); crypto.getRandomValues(iv);
  const ct = await subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: aad({ groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) }, key, enc.encode(plaintext));
  return { v: CIPHER_VERSION, iv: b64(iv), ct: b64(new Uint8Array(ct)) };
}
export async function decryptGroupMessage({ rawKey, envelope, groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) {
  if (!envelope || envelope.v !== CIPHER_VERSION) throw new Error('unsupported envelope');
  const key = await importKey(rawKey);
  const pt = await subtle.decrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: unb64(envelope.iv), additionalData: aad({ groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) }, key, unb64(envelope.ct));
  return dec.decode(pt);
}

FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupKeyDistribution.js
=========================================

import { toHex } from './groupCipher';
import { recordGrant, getGrantedEpochsFor } from './groupKeyStore';
export async function distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey, senderPubkey, groupId, epoch, keyBytes, recipients, publish, extraEpochs = [] }) {
  const { nip44, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  await Promise.allSettled(recipients.map(async (recipientPubkey) => {
    const granted = await getGrantedEpochsFor(groupId, recipientPubkey);
    const bundles = [];
    if (!granted.has(epoch)) bundles.push({ ep: epoch, k: toHex(keyBytes) });
    for (const e of extraEpochs) { if (!granted.has(e.epoch)) bundles.push({ ep: e.epoch, k: toHex(e.bytes) }); }
    if (!bundles.length) return;
    const payload = { v: 1, t: 'gkey', gid: groupId, by: senderPubkey, at: now, keys: bundles };
    const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(senderPrivkey, recipientPubkey);
    const wrapped = nip44.v2.encrypt(JSON.stringify(payload), shared);
    const event = finalizeEvent({ kind: 4, created_at: now, tags: [['p', recipientPubkey]], content: wrapped }, senderPrivkey);
    try { await publish(event); for (const b of bundles) await recordGrant(groupId, recipientPubkey, b.ep); } catch (e) {}
  }));
}

FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupKeyStore.js

import { db, currentOwner } from '../../db';
import { fromHex, toHex } from './groupCipher';
const ready = () => !!currentOwner();
export async function getGroupKey(groupId, epoch) {
  if (!ready()) return null;
  const row = await db.groupKeys.where('[groupId+epoch]').equals([groupId, epoch]).first();
  return row ? fromHex(row.keyHex) : null;
}
export async function getCurrentGroupKey(groupId) {
  if (!ready()) return null;
  const rows = await db.groupKeys.where('groupId').equals(groupId).toArray();
  if (!rows.length) return null;
  const latest = rows.reduce((a, b) => (a.epoch > b.epoch ? a : b));
  return { epoch: latest.epoch, bytes: fromHex(latest.keyHex), obtainedAt: latest.obtainedAt };
}
export async function getAllGroupKeys(groupId) {
  if (!ready()) return [];
  const rows = await db.groupKeys.where('groupId').equals(groupId).toArray();
  return rows.map(r => ({ epoch: r.epoch, bytes: fromHex(r.keyHex) }));
}
export async function putGroupKey(groupId, epoch, raw, meta = {}) {
  if (!ready()) return;
  await db.transaction('rw', db.groupKeys, async () => {
    await db.groupKeys.put({ groupId, epoch, keyHex: toHex(raw), isCurrent: true, obtainedAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), ...meta });
    await db.groupKeys.where('groupId').equals(groupId).modify(row => { if (row.epoch !== epoch) row.isCurrent = false; });
  });
}
export async function hasGroupKey(groupId, epoch) { return !!(await getGroupKey(groupId, epoch)); }
export async function getGrantedEpochsFor(groupId, pubkey) {
  if (!ready()) return new Set();
  const rows = await db.groupKeyGrants.where('[groupId+pubkey+epoch]').between([groupId, pubkey, -Infinity], [groupId, pubkey, Infinity]).toArray();
  return new Set(rows.map(r => r.epoch));
}
export async function recordGrant(groupId, pubkey, epoch) {
  if (!ready()) return;
  await db.groupKeyGrants.put({ groupId, pubkey, epoch, sentAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) });
}

=========================================
FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupMessageCodec.js
=========================================

import { getCurrentGroupKey, getGroupKey } from './groupKeyStore';
import { encryptGroupMessage, decryptGroupMessage } from './groupCipher';
const KEY_WAIT_MS = 5000; const KEY_POLL_MS = 200;
async function waitForKey(groupId) {
  const start = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - start < KEY_WAIT_MS) { const k = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId); if (k) return k; await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, KEY_POLL_MS)); }
  return null;
}
export async function encodeGroupPayload({ groupId, payload, senderPubkey, waitForKey: wfk = false }) {
  let current = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
  if (!current && wfk) current = await waitForKey(groupId);
  if (!current) return { pending: true };
  const envelope = await encryptGroupMessage({ rawKey: current.bytes, plaintext: JSON.stringify(payload), groupId, epoch: current.epoch, senderPubkey });
  return { content: JSON.stringify(envelope), epoch: current.epoch, tags: [['ep', String(current.epoch)]] };
}
export async function decodeGroupEvent({ event, groupId }) {
  const epTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'ep');
  if (!epTag) {
    try { return { plaintext: event.content, parsed: JSON.parse(event.content), legacy: true }; }
    catch { return { plaintext: event.content, parsed: { type: 'text', message: event.content }, legacy: true }; }
  }
  const epoch = parseInt(epTag[1], 10);
  const key = await getGroupKey(groupId, epoch);
  if (!key) return { pending: true, epoch };
  try {
    const env = JSON.parse(event.content);
    const plaintext = await decryptGroupMessage({ rawKey: key, envelope: env, groupId, epoch, senderPubkey: event.pubkey });
    return { plaintext, parsed: JSON.parse(plaintext), epoch };
  } catch (e) { return { error: 'decrypt-failed', epoch, reason: e.message }; }
}

  'wss://groups.aegispayhq.com'

  'wss://relay.primal.net',
  'wss://relay.vertexlab.io',
  'wss://relay.noswhere.com'
];

export const queryWithTimeout = (pool, relays, filter, timeoutMs = 4000) => {
  return Promise.race([
    pool.querySync(relays, filter).catch(() => []),
    new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(() => resolve([]), timeoutMs))
  ]);
};

// src/lib/myGroups.js
// Per-pubkey joined-group list. Replaces the global `cipher_my_groups`
// localStorage key that caused cross-account group leakage.

const PREFIX = 'cipher_my_groups_';

export function getMyGroups(pubkey) {
  if (!pubkey) return [];
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PREFIX + pubkey.slice(0, 16));
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
  } catch { return []; }
}

export function setMyGroups(pubkey, groups) {
  if (!pubkey) return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(PREFIX + pubkey.slice(0, 16), JSON.stringify(groups || []));
  } catch {}
}

// One-time: drop the legacy unscoped key so it stops polluting any account.
try { localStorage.removeItem('cipher_my_groups'); } catch {}

FILE: src/useProfiles.js

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { db, saveProfile } from './db'

let listeners = new Set();

export function updateProfileCache(pubkey, data) {
  saveProfile({ pubkey, ...data }).then(() => {
    listeners.forEach(listener => listener());
  });
}

export function useProfiles(pubkeys) {
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState({})

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadLocal = async () => {
      const localData = {};
      for (const pk of pubkeys) {
        if (!pk) continue;
        const p = await db.profiles.get(pk);
        if (p) localData[pk] = p;
      }
      setProfiles(prev => ({ ...prev, ...localData }));
    };
    if (pubkeys.length > 0) loadLocal();
  }, [pubkeys.join(',')])

  useEffect(() => {
    const listener = () => {
      const loadAll = async () => {
        const localData = {};
        for (const pk of pubkeys) {
          const p = await db.profiles.get(pk);
          if (p) localData[pk] = p;
        }
        setProfiles(localData);
      };
      loadAll();
    }
    listeners.add(listener)
    return () => listeners.delete(listener)
  }, [pubkeys.join(',')])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pubkeys || pubkeys.length === 0) return

    const fetchProfiles = async () => {
      const validPubkeys = pubkeys.filter(pk => typeof pk === 'string' && pk.length >= 8)
      const missing = [];
      for (const pk of validPubkeys) {
        const exists = await db.profiles.get(pk);
        if (!exists) missing.push(pk);
      }
      
      if (missing.length === 0) return

      try {
        const { SimplePool } = await import('nostr-tools')
        const pool = new SimplePool({ enablePing: true, enableReconnect: true })
        const relays = ['wss://purplepag.es', 'wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.primal.net']

        const events = await Promise.race([
          pool.querySync(relays, { kinds: [0], authors: missing }),
          new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(() => resolve([]), 4000))
        ]);
        
        // If no profiles found, DON'T create fake fallback profiles
        if (events.length === 0) return; 

        for (const ev of events) {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(ev.content)
            const profileData = {
              pubkey: ev.pubkey,
              name: data.display_name || data.name || ev.pubkey.slice(0, 8),
              displayName: data.display_name || data.name || ev.pubkey.slice(0, 8),
              picture: data.picture || null,
              about: data.about || ''
            }
            await saveProfile(profileData);
          } catch (e) {}
        }
        
        listeners.forEach(l => l());
      } catch (err) { console.error("Profile fetch error", err) }
    }

    fetchProfiles()
  }, [pubkeys.join(',')])

  return profiles
}

function renderMessage(text) {
  if (!text) return null;
  const parts = text.split(/(\|\|.*?\|\|)/g);
  return parts.map((part, i) => {
    if (part.startsWith('||') && part.endsWith('||')) {
      return <span key={i} onClick={(e) => { e.currentTarget.classList.remove('blur-sm', 'text-transparent'); e.currentTarget.classList.add('text-white'); }} className="bg-surfaceLight px-1 rounded cursor-pointer blur-sm text-transparent transition-all select-none">{part.slice(2, -2)}</span>
    }
    const boldParts = part.split(/(\*\*.*?\*\*)/g);
    return boldParts.map((bp, j) => {
      if (bp.startsWith('**') && bp.endsWith('**')) return <strong key={`${i}-${j}`}>{bp.slice(2, -2)}</strong>
      return bp
    })
  })
}


// ✅ GLOBAL POOL: Lives outside React so StrictMode doesn't kill it
// Revised Global Pool Logic

const copyToClipboard = async (text) => {
  console.log("[Clipboard] Attempting to copy:", text);
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      console.log("[Clipboard] Success via modern API");
      return true;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[Clipboard] Modern API failed, trying fallback", err);
  }

  // Fallback for mobile/HTTP
  try {
    const textArea = document.createElement("textarea");
    textArea.value = text;
    textArea.style.position = "fixed";
    textArea.style.top = "0";
    textArea.style.left = "0";
    textArea.style.opacity = "0";
    document.body.appendChild(textArea);
    textArea.focus();
    textArea.select();
    const successful = document.execCommand('copy');
    document.body.removeChild(textArea);
    console.log("[Clipboard] Fallback success:", successful);
    return successful;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[Clipboard] Fallback failed", err);
    return false;
  }
};
