import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import {  
  User, Plus, AlertCircle, RefreshCw, 
  Lock, ChevronRight, ArrowLeft, Copy, CreditCard, Camera
, BarChart3 , Moon, Sun} from 'lucide-react'
import { uploadToBlossom } from '@nostr-wot/blossom'
import { useProfiles, updateProfileCache } from './useProfiles'

import { useChatStore } from './store/chatStore';

import VendorDashboard from './components/chat/VendorDashboard';

export default function Wallet({ identity }) {
  const { isVendor, setIsVendor, theme, setTheme } = useChatStore();
  const [showPanicConfirm, setShowPanicConfirm] = useState(false);
  const panicWipeEnabled = useChatStore((s) => s.panicWipeEnabled);
  const setPanicWipeEnabled = useChatStore((s) => s.setPanicWipeEnabled);
  const profiles = useProfiles([identity.pk])
  const defaultName = identity.npub ? identity.npub.slice(0, 10) : 'User'
  
  const myProfile = profiles[identity.pk] || { name: defaultName, displayName: defaultName, picture: null, about: '' }

  const [view, setView] = useState('main') 

  // Profile state
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('') 
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('')
  const [picture, setPicture] = useState('')
  const [about, setAbout] = useState('')
  
  // Product state
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('')
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('')
  const [price, setPrice] = useState('')
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState('USD')
  const [image, setImage] = useState('')

  const [btcAddress, setBtcAddress] = useState(localStorage.getItem('cipher_btc_address') || '')
  const [xmrAddress, setXmrAddress] = useState(localStorage.getItem('cipher_xmr_address') || '')

  const [status, setStatus] = useState('')
  const [isPublishing, setIsPublishing] = useState(false)
  const [uploadingImage, setUploadingImage] = useState(false)
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (myProfile.name) setUsername(myProfile.name)
    if (myProfile.displayName) setDisplayName(myProfile.displayName)
    if (myProfile.picture) setPicture(myProfile.picture)
    if (myProfile.about) setAbout(myProfile.about)
  }, [myProfile.name, myProfile.displayName, myProfile.picture, myProfile.about])

  const handleImageUpload = async (e, type) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
    if (!file) return

    setUploadingImage(true)
    setStatus('Uploading via Blossom...')

    try {
      const blob = await uploadToBlossom(file, {
        signer: {
          getPublicKey: async () => identity.pk,
          signEvent: async (event) => {
            const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools')
            return finalizeEvent(event, identity.sk)
          }
        },
      })

      if (type === 'profile') {
        setPicture(blob.url)
        // Auto-save profile if they just changed the picture from the main screen
        if (view === 'main') {
          await saveProfile(null, blob.url)
        }
      }
      if (type === 'product') setImage(blob.url)
      
      setStatus('Image uploaded!')
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
      useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'info', message: `Upload failed: ${err.message}` });
    } finally {
      setUploadingImage(false)
      setTimeout(() => setStatus(''), 3000)
    }
  }

  const copyNpub = () => {
    if (identity.npub) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(identity.npub)
      setCopied(true); setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000)
    }
  }

  const saveProfile = async (e, overridePicture = null) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault()
    
    // Save crypto addresses locally
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_btc_address', btcAddress);
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_xmr_address', xmrAddress);
    if (!displayName) return useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'info', message: "Display Name is required" });
    
    setIsPublishing(true); setStatus('Saving Profile...')
    const picToSave = overridePicture || picture
    
    try {
      const { SimplePool, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const pool = new SimplePool()
      const relays = ['wss://relay.primal.net', ]

      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 0, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), tags: [],
        content: JSON.stringify({ 
          name: username, 
          display_name: displayName, 
          about, 
          picture: picToSave 
        })
      }, identity.sk)

      const publishPromises = pool.publish(relays, ev)
      const timeout = new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 3000))
      await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled(publishPromises), timeout])
      
      // ✅ NEW: Publish Kind 10003 (Unique Username Registry)
      if (username) {
        try {
          const usernameEv = finalizeEvent({
            kind: 10003, 
            created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
            tags: [
              ['d', 'username'],
              ['t', 'cipher_username_' + username],
              ['t', 'cipher_username'],
              ['username', username]
            ],
            content: ''
          }, identity.sk)
          
          const usernameRelays = ['wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://groups.aegispayhq.com']
          const usernamePublish = pool.publish(usernameRelays, usernameEv)
          await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled(usernamePublish), timeout])
          console.log('[Username] Successfully published kind 10003 for @' + username)
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('[Username] Failed to publish username:', e)
        }
      }
      
      
      // ✅ Publish Kind 10003 (Unique Username Registry)
      if (username) {
        try {
          const usernameEv = finalizeEvent({
            kind: 10003, 
            created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
            tags: [
              ['d', 'username'],
              ['t', 'cipher_username_' + username],
              ['t', 'cipher_username'],
              ['username', username]
            ],
            content: ''
          }, identity.sk)
          
          const usernameRelays = ['wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://groups.aegispayhq.com']
          const usernamePublish = pool.publish(usernameRelays, usernameEv)
          await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled(usernamePublish), timeout])
          console.log('[Username] Successfully published kind 10003 for @' + username)
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('[Username] Failed to publish username:', e)
        }
      }

      updateProfileCache(identity.pk, { name: username, displayName, picture: picToSave, about })
      
      setStatus('Profile Saved!')
      setView('main')
    } catch (err) { setStatus('Error: ' + err.message) } 
    finally { setIsPublishing(false); setTimeout(() => setStatus(''), 3000) }
  }

  const publishProduct = async () => {
    if (!title || !price) return useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'info', message: "Title and Price required" });
    setIsPublishing(true); setStatus('Publishing...')
    try {
      const { SimplePool, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const pool = new SimplePool()
      const relays = ['wss://relay.primal.net', ]
      const dTag = Date.now().toString()
      
      const tags = [
        ['d', dTag], ['title', title], ['price', price, currency], 
        ['image', image || 'https://via.placeholder.com/400'], ['stock', '1'],
        ['status', 'active'], ['published_at', Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000).toString()],
        ['t', 'cipher_marketplace'] // <--- FIX: Added the tag Explore looks for
      ]
      if (btcAddress) tags.push(['payment', `btc:${btcAddress}`])
      if (xmrAddress) tags.push(['payment', `xmr:${xmrAddress}`])
      
      const content = JSON.stringify({ name: title, description, price, currency, image })
      const ev = finalizeEvent({ kind: 30402, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), tags, content }, identity.sk)
      
      const publishPromises = pool.publish(relays, ev)
      const timeout = new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 3000))
      await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled(publishPromises), timeout])
      
      const newProduct = {
        id: ev.id, name: title, description, price, currency,
        image: image || 'https://via.placeholder.com/400', stock: '1',
        vendorPubkey: identity.pk, created_at: ev.created_at,
        vendorSettings: { btcAddress: btcAddress || null, xmrAddress: xmrAddress || null }
      }
        
      const cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cipher_cached_products') || '[]')
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_cached_products', JSON.stringify([newProduct, ...cached.filter(p => p.id !== ev.id)]))

      setStatus('Product Published!')
      setTitle(''); setDescription(''); setPrice(''); setImage('')
      setView('main')
    } catch (err) { setStatus('Error: ' + err.message) } 
    finally { setIsPublishing(false); setTimeout(() => setStatus(''), 3000) }
  }

  const savePayments = () => {
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_btc', btcAddress)
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_xmr', xmrAddress)
    setStatus('Addresses saved!'); setTimeout(() => setStatus(''), 2000)
    setView('main')
  }

    if (view === 'vendor') {
    return <VendorDashboard identity={identity} products={JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cipher_my_products') || '[]')} onBack={() => setView('main')} />;
  }

  // --- SUB-SCREEN: EDIT PROFILE ---
  if (view === 'edit_profile') {
    return (
      <div className="h-full p-4 animate-slide-up pb-24 flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
          <button onClick={() => setView('main')} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-black dark:text-white/50 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors">
            <ArrowLeft size={20}/>
          </button>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Edit Profile</h2>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-8">
          <div className="relative">
            {picture ? (
              <img src={picture} alt="avatar" className="w-24 h-24 rounded-xl object-cover border-2 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-lg" />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-xl bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-lg">
                <User size={32} className="text-black dark:text-white/30" />
              </div>
            )}
            
            <input 
              type="file" 
              accept="image/*" 
              onChange={(e) => handleImageUpload(e, 'profile')} 
              className="hidden" 
              id="profile-upload" 
            />
            
            <label htmlFor="profile-upload" className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 p-2 bg-white text-black rounded-xl border-4 border-base cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-200 transition-transform active:scale-95">
              {uploadingImage ? <RefreshCw size={14} className="animate-spin"/> : <Camera size={14} />}
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={saveProfile} className="space-y-5">
          
          <div className="bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-black dark:text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">Payment Addresses</h3>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs text-black dark:text-white/60 mb-1">Bitcoin (BTC) Address</label>
                <input 
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="bc1q..."
                  value={btcAddress}
                  onChange={(e) => setBtcAddress(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-2 text-sm text-black dark:text-white placeholder-white/20 focus:border-white/30 outline-none"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs text-black dark:text-white/60 mb-1">Monero (XMR) Address</label>
                <input 
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="4..."
                  value={xmrAddress}
                  onChange={(e) => setXmrAddress(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-2 text-sm text-black dark:text-white placeholder-white/20 focus:border-white/30 outline-none"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Locked Username Field - Clean Flexbox */}
          <div>
            <div className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className="text-black dark:text-white/30 text-lg">@</span>
              <span className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white/50 font-mono tracking-wide">{username}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/30 mt-1.5 ml-1">Usernames are permanent and cannot be changed.</p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 ml-1 mb-1.5 block">Display Name</label>
            <input 
              placeholder="e.g. Alice's Shop" 
              value={displayName} 
              onChange={e => setDisplayName(e.target.value)} 
              className="w-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm text-black dark:text-white placeholder-white/20 focus:border-white/30 outline-none transition-colors" 
            />
          </div>
          
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 ml-1 mb-1.5 block">Bio</label>
            <textarea 
              placeholder="Tell buyers about your shop..." 
              value={about} 
              onChange={e => setAbout(e.target.value)} 
              className="w-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm text-black dark:text-white placeholder-white/20 focus:border-white/30 outline-none resize-none h-24 transition-colors" 
            />
          </div>
          
          <button type="submit" disabled={isPublishing || uploadingImage} className="w-full py-4 bg-white text-black rounded-xl font-bold text-sm mt-2 flex justify-center items-center gap-2 transition-transform active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50">
            {isPublishing ? <RefreshCw size={16} className="animate-spin"/> : 'Save Profile'}
          </button>
        </form>
        <div className="mt-8 border-t border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 pt-6">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-red-400 mb-2">Danger Zone</h3>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 p-4 rounded-xl">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-400">Panic Wipe (Shake)</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-red-400/60">Violently shake phone to wipe all data instantly.</p>
            </div>
            <input 
              type="checkbox" 
              checked={panicWipeEnabled}
              onChange={(e) => setPanicWipeEnabled(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-6 h-6 rounded accent-red-500 cursor-pointer"
            />
          </div>
        </div>


        </div>
    )
  }

  // --- SUB-SCREEN: ADD PRODUCT ---
  if (view === 'product') {
    return (
      <div className="h-full p-4 animate-slide-up flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pb-24">
        <button onClick={() => setView('main')} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-black dark:text-white/50 text-sm mb-6 hover:text-black dark:text-white"><ArrowLeft size={16}/> Back</button>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-6">List New Product</h2>
        
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input placeholder="Product Title" value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm focus:border-white/30 outline-none" />
          <textarea placeholder="Description" value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm focus:border-white/30 outline-none resize-none h-24" />
          
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl">
            {image ? (
              <img src={image} alt="product" className="w-16 h-16 rounded-lg object-cover" />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-black flex items-center justify-center">
                <Camera size={24} className="text-gray-400 dark:text-white/20" />
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex-1">
              <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={(e) => handleImageUpload(e, 'product')} className="hidden" id="product-upload" />
              <label htmlFor="product-upload" className="py-2 px-4 bg-white/10 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-borderDark transition-colors inline-block">
                {uploadingImage ? 'Uploading...' : 'Choose Photo'}
              </label>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <input placeholder="Price" type="number" value={price} onChange={e => setPrice(e.target.value)} className="flex-1 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm focus:border-white/30 outline-none" />
            <select value={currency} onChange={e => setCurrency(e.target.value)} className="bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl px-4 text-sm focus:border-white/30 outline-none">
              <option value="USD">USD</option><option value="BTC">BTC</option><option value="XMR">XMR</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          
          <button onClick={publishProduct} disabled={isPublishing || uploadingImage} className="w-full py-4 bg-white text-black rounded-xl font-bold text-sm mt-4 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
            {isPublishing ? <RefreshCw size={16} className="animate-spin"/> : 'Publish to Store'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // --- SUB-SCREEN: PAYMENTS ---
  if (view === 'payments') {
    return (
      <div className="h-full p-4 animate-slide-up flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pb-24">
        <button onClick={() => setView('main')} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-black dark:text-white/50 text-sm mb-6 hover:text-black dark:text-white"><ArrowLeft size={16}/> Back</button>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-6">Payment Addresses</h2>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input placeholder="Bitcoin (BTC)" value={btcAddress} onChange={e => setBtcAddress(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm focus:border-white/30 outline-none" />
          <input placeholder="Monero (XMR)" value={xmrAddress} onChange={e => setXmrAddress(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm focus:border-white/30 outline-none" />
          <button onClick={savePayments} className="w-full py-4 bg-white text-black rounded-xl font-bold text-sm mt-4">Save Addresses</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // --- MAIN PROFILE TAB ---
  return (
    <div className="h-full p-4 animate-fade-in flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pb-24">
      {status && <div className="fixed top-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-white text-black px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-lg z-50 animate-bounce">{status}</div>}

      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-6 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-3xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 mb-6">
        <div className="relative mb-4">
          {/* MAKE AVATAR CLICKABLE TO CHANGE PFP */}
          <input 
            type="file" 
            accept="image/*" 
            onChange={(e) => handleImageUpload(e, 'profile')} 
            className="hidden" 
            id="main-avatar-upload" 
          />
          <label htmlFor="main-avatar-upload" className="cursor-pointer group relative block">
            {myProfile.picture ? (
              <img src={myProfile.picture} alt="avatar" className="w-24 h-24 rounded-xl object-cover border-2 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 group-hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity" />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-xl bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 group-hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity">
                <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white/50">{myProfile.displayName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            
            {/* Camera Overlay */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/40 rounded-xl opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
              {uploadingImage ? <RefreshCw size={24} className="text-black dark:text-white animate-spin" /> : <Camera size={24} className="text-black dark:text-white" />}
            </div>
          </label>
        </div>
        
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{myProfile.displayName}</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40 mb-2">@{myProfile.name}</p>
        
        {myProfile.about && <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 mt-1 px-8 text-center">{myProfile.about}</p>}
        
        <button onClick={copyNpub} className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors">
          {copied ? 'Copied!' : `${identity.npub?.slice(0, 12)}...${identity.npub?.slice(-4)}`}
          <Copy size={12} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-2xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 overflow-hidden">
        
        {/* EDIT PROFILE MENU ITEM */}
        <button onClick={() => setView('edit_profile')} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors border-b border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50">
          <div className="p-2 bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 rounded-lg"><User size={20}/></div>
          <div className="flex-1 text-left">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Edit Profile</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40">Change your display name and bio</p>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight size={18} className="text-gray-400 dark:text-white/20" />
        </button>

        {isVendor && (
<button onClick={() => setView('product')} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-white/10">
          <div className="p-2 bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 rounded-lg"><Plus size={20}/></div>
          <div className="flex-1 text-left">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">My Store</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40">Publish a new product listing</p>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight size={18} className="text-gray-400 dark:text-white/20" />
        </button>
)}
          {isVendor && (
<button onClick={() => setView('vendor')} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-white/10">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400">
              <BarChart3 size={20} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 text-left">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-black dark:text-white">Vendor Dashboard</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50">View stats, sales, and badges</p>
            </div>
            <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className="text-black dark:text-white/30"><path d="m9 18 6-6-6-6"/></svg>
          </button>
)}
        

        {isVendor && (
<button onClick={() => setView('payments')} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-white/10">
          <div className="p-2 bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 rounded-lg"><CreditCard size={20}/></div>
          <div className="flex-1 text-left">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Payment Addresses</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40">Configure your BTC and XMR wallets</p>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight size={18} className="text-gray-400 dark:text-white/20" />
        </button>
)}

        <button onClick={() => setTheme(theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark')} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-white/10">
          <div className="p-2 bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400 rounded-lg">
            {theme === 'dark' ? <Sun size={20}/> : <Moon size={20}/>}
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 text-left">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-black dark:text-white">Toggle Theme</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Switch between Light and Dark mode</p>
          </div>
        </button>
        
<button onClick={identity.lockApp} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-white/10">
          <div className="p-2 bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400 rounded-lg"><Lock size={20}/></div>
          <div className="flex-1 text-left">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Lock App</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40">Securely lock Cipher</p>
          </div>
        </button>

        <button onClick={() => { if (window.confirm("This will wipe local data. Make sure you saved your 12-word phrase! Proceed?")) identity.panicWipe() }} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-red-500/10 transition-colors">
          <div className="p-2 bg-red-500/20 text-red-500 rounded-lg"><AlertCircle size={20}/></div>
          <div className="flex-1 text-left">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-500">Panic Wipe</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-red-500/60">Destroy all local data instantly</p>
          </div>
        </button>
      </div>
    
      
      <button 
        onClick={() => {
          setIsVendor(!isVendor);
          useChatStore.getState().setToast({ 
            type: 'success', 
            message: !isVendor ? 'Vendor tools unlocked!' : 'Switched to Buyer mode.' 
          });
        }}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-between bg-[#111] border border-white/10 p-4 rounded-2xl hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors mt-4 mb-8"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400">
            <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2"><path d="M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H6a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/><line x1="19" y1="8" x2="19" y2="14"/><line x1="22" y1="11" x2="16" y2="11"/></svg>
          </div>
          <div className="text-left">
            <p className="text-black dark:text-white font-bold">{isVendor ? 'Switch to Buyer Mode' : 'Become a Vendor'}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50">{isVendor ? 'Hide store and dashboard' : 'Unlock store, dashboard & payments'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </button>

      {showPanicConfirm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-red-500/30 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 shadow-2xl">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-red-500 mb-2">Panic Wipe</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white/70 mb-6">This will instantly destroy all local data on this device. Make sure you have saved your 12-word recovery phrase!</p>
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => setShowPanicConfirm(false)} className="flex-1 py-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-white/5 rounded-xl font-bold transition-colors">Cancel</button>
              <button onClick={async () => {
                setShowPanicConfirm(false);
                // Execute the actual wipe logic here
                localStorage.clear();
                indexedDB.databases().then(dbs => dbs.forEach(db => indexedDB.deleteDatabase(db.name)));
                window.location.reload();
              }} className="flex-1 py-3 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-black dark:text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition-colors">Wipe It</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
</div>
  )
}

// src/db.js
import Dexie from 'dexie';

let _current = null;
let _currentOwner = null;
let _warnedOnce = false;

function makeDB(name) {
  const d = new Dexie(name);
  d.version(8).stores({
    messages: 'id, chatId, pubkey, timestamp, clientId, type',
    profiles: 'pubkey, name, displayName',
    groups: 'id, name',
    chatReads: 'chatId',
    groupKeys: '++id, &[groupId+epoch], groupId, epoch, isCurrent, obtainedAt',
    groupKeyGrants: '++id, &[groupId+pubkey+epoch], groupId, pubkey, epoch, sentAt',
    groupMemberSigs: '&groupId, members, updatedAt',
  });
  return d;
}

export function openDBFor(pubkey) {
  if (!pubkey) return null;
  localStorage.setItem('cipher_db_owner', pubkey);
  if (_currentOwner === pubkey && _current) return _current;
  if (_current) { try { _current.close(); } catch (_) {} }
  const name = 'CipherDB_v6_' + pubkey.slice(0, 16);
  _current = makeDB(name);
  _currentOwner = pubkey;
  return _current;
}

// Restore DB connection if HMR reloads this module
try {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('cipher_db_owner');
  if (saved) {
    const name = 'CipherDB_v7_' + saved.slice(0, 16);
    _current = makeDB(name);
    _currentOwner = saved;
  }
} catch (e) {}

export function closeDB() {
  if (_current) { try { _current.close(); } catch (_) {} }
  _current = null;
  _currentOwner = null;
}

export function currentOwner() { return _currentOwner; }

// Safe stub returned when accessed before openDBFor().
// Reads return empty, writes no-op. Prevents pre-login crashes.
const stubTable = () => ({
  toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]),
  get: () => Promise.resolve(undefined),
  put: () => Promise.resolve(),
  add: () => Promise.resolve(),
  clear: () => Promise.resolve(),
  delete: () => Promise.resolve(),
  count: () => Promise.resolve(0),
  where: () => ({
    equals: () => ({
      sortBy: () => Promise.resolve([]),
      toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]),
      first: () => Promise.resolve(undefined),
      modify: () => Promise.resolve(0),
    }),
    between: () => ({ toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]) }),
    sortBy: () => Promise.resolve([]),
    toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]),
  }),
  filter: () => ({ toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]) }),
});

export const db = new Proxy({}, {
  get(_t, prop) {
    if (!_current) {
      if (!_warnedOnce) {
        _warnedOnce = true;
        console.warn('[db] accessed before openDBFor — returning empty stubs');
      }
      if (['messages', 'chatReads', 'profiles', 'groups', 'groupKeys', 'groupKeyGrants', 'groupMemberSigs'].includes(prop)) {
        return stubTable();
      }
      return () => Promise.resolve();
    }
    const v = _current[prop];
    return typeof v === 'function' ? v.bind(_current) : v;
  },
});

export const saveMessage = async (chatId, msg) => {
  try { await db.messages.put({ ...msg, chatId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
};

export const getMessages = async (chatId) => {
  return await db.messages.where('chatId').equals(chatId).sortBy('timestamp');
};

export const saveProfile = async (profile) => {
  try { await db.profiles.put(profile); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
};

export const getProfile = async (pubkey) => {
  return await db.profiles.get(pubkey);
};

export const searchProfilesLocal = async (query) => {
  const lowerQuery = query.toLowerCase();
  return await db.profiles.filter(p =>
    (p.name && p.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery)) ||
    (p.displayName && p.displayName.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery))
  ).toArray();
};

FILE: src/components/chat/MyProfileModal.jsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, groupRelays } from '../../lib/relays';

export default function MyProfileModal({ identity }) {
  const showMyProfile = useChatStore((s) => s.showMyProfile);
  const setShowMyProfile = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowMyProfile);
  const myName = useChatStore((s) => s.myName);
  const setMyName = useChatStore((s) => s.setMyName);
  const myUsername = useChatStore((s) => s.myUsername);
  const setMyUsername = useChatStore((s) => s.setMyUsername);
  const myAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.myAbout);
  const setMyAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.setMyAbout);
  const setProfiles = useChatStore((s) => s.setProfiles);
  const setToast = useChatStore((s) => s.setToast);

  const [lockedUsername, setLockedUsername] = useState(
    () => localStorage.getItem('cipher_my_username') || ''
  );
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  if (!showMyProfile) return null;

  const handleSave = async () => {
    try {
      setSaving(true);
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      // Publish Kind 0
      const profileEv = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 0,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [],
        content: JSON.stringify({
          name: myName,
          display_name: myName,
          about: myAbout,
          nip05: lockedUsername || myUsername
        })
      }, identity.sk);
      await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, profileEv));

      // Publish Kind 10003 only if not locked yet
      if (!lockedUsername && myUsername) {
        const usernameEv = finalizeEvent({
          kind: 10003,
          created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
          tags: [
            ['d', 'username'],
            ['t', 'cipher_username_' + myUsername],
            ['t', 'cipher_username'],
            ['username', myUsername]
          ],
          content: ''
        }, identity.sk);
        const allPublishRelays = [...generalRelays, ...groupRelays];
        await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(allPublishRelays, usernameEv));
        localStorage.setItem('cipher_my_username', myUsername);
        setLockedUsername(myUsername);
      }

      setProfiles((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        [identity.pk]: {
          pubkey: identity.pk,
          name: myName,
          displayName: myName,
          about: myAbout,
          username: lockedUsername || myUsername
        }
      }));
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Profile updated!' });
      setShowMyProfile(false);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Error: ' + e.message });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#111]/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 relative">
        <button onClick={() => setShowMyProfile(false)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-white/50 hover:text-white">✕</button>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Your Profile</h2>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm text-white/60 mb-1">Display Name</label>
            <input type="text" value={myName} onChange={(e) => setMyName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Your name" className="w-full bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm text-white/60 mb-1">Username (@handle)</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <span className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-white/40">@</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={lockedUsername || myUsername}
                onChange={(e) => setMyUsername(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''))}
                placeholder="alice"
                disabled={!!lockedUsername}
                className={`w-full bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 pl-8 text-white ${lockedUsername ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}`}
              />
              {lockedUsername && (
                <span className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-white/40 text-xs flex items-center gap-1">🔒 Locked</span>
              )}
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-1">
              {lockedUsername ? 'Usernames cannot be changed after they are set.' : 'Pick carefully! You can only set this once.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm text-white/60 mb-1">Bio</label>
            <input type="text" value={myAbout} onChange={(e) => setMyAbout(e.target.value)} placeholder="Tell us about yourself" className="w-full bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 text-white" />
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg mt-2 disabled:opacity-50">
            {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Profile'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Package, MapPin, CheckCircle2, Truck, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function OrderManagerModal({ order, onUpdateStatus, onClose }) {
  const [updating, setUpdating] = useState(false);

  if (!order) return null;
  const d = order.data;
  console.log("🟢 Modal Order Data:", d);

  const handleUpdate = async (status) => {
    setUpdating(true);
    await onUpdateStatus(order, status);
    setUpdating(false);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fade-in">

          <X size={20} />
        </button>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
          <Package className="text-blue-400" size={24} />
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white">Order Details</h2>
        </div>

          <div>

            </div>

          <button

            disabled={updating || d.status === 'accepted'}

          >
            <CheckCircle2 size={18} /> Accept Order
          </button>

          >
            <XCircle size={18} /> Cancel Order

import React from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function CreateGroupModal({ createGroup }) {
  const showCreateGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.showCreateGroup);
  const setShowCreateGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowCreateGroup);
  const newGroupName = useChatStore((s) => s.newGroupName);
  const setNewGroupName = useChatStore((s) => s.setNewGroupName);
  const newGroupAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.newGroupAbout);
  const setNewGroupAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.setNewGroupAbout);
  const isChannel = useChatStore((s) => s.isChannel);
  const setIsChannel = useChatStore((s) => s.setIsChannel);
  const loading = useChatStore((s) => s.loading);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">

          </div>

            <label htmlFor="isChannel" className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white/80">Create as Channel</label>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" disabled={loading || newGroupName.trim().length === 0} className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-black dark:text-white font-semibold py-3 rounded-lg transition-colors">
            {loading ? 'Creating...' : 'Create'}

import React from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Search, Megaphone, Users } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function SearchView({ ToastUI, handleSearch, joinGroupById, openChat }) {
  const searchQuery = useChatStore((s) => s.searchQuery);
  const setSearchQuery = useChatStore((s) => s.setSearchQuery);
  const searchResults = useChatStore((s) => s.searchResults);
  const isSearching = useChatStore((s) => s.isSearching);
  const setView = useChatStore((s) => s.setView);

  const handleResultClick = (res) => {
    if (res.type === 'user') {
      openChat('dm', res.pubkey);
    } else if (res.type === 'group') {
      // Save locally so it doesn't disappear
      const localGroups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cipher_my_groups') || '[]');
      if (!localGroups.find(g => g.id === res.id)) {
        localGroups.push({ id: res.id, name: res.name, isChannel: res.isChannel, creator: res.creator });
        localStorage.setItem('cipher_my_groups', JSON.stringify(localGroups));
      }
      openChat('group', res.id, res);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-white dark:bg-black">
      <ToastUI />
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button onClick={() => setView('list')} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-black dark:text-white/60">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} />
          </button>
          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] rounded-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800">
            <Search size={16} className="text-black dark:text-white/40 mr-2" />
            <textarea
              autoFocus
              rows={1}
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); handleSearch(); } }}
              placeholder="Search @users, groups, or paste npub/hex..."
              className="bg-transparent text-sm text-black dark:text-white outline-none w-full resize-none pt-1"
            />
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleSearch} className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-400">Search</button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            id="join-group-id"
            placeholder="Paste Group ID to join directly..."
            className="flex-1 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm text-black dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          />
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              const gid = document.getElementById('join-group-id').value.trim();
              if (gid) joinGroupById(gid);
            }}
            className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-black dark:text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg"
          >
            Join
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 p-4 space-y-3">
        {isSearching && <p className="text-center text-black dark:text-white/50 animate-pulse">Searching relays...</p>}
        {searchResults.map((res, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            onClick={() => handleResultClick(res)}
            className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer"
          >
            {res.type === 'user' ? (
              res.picture
                ? <img src={res.picture} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full" />
                : <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] flex items-center justify-center font-bold">{res.name?.charAt(0)}</div>
            ) : (
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                {res.isChannel ? <Megaphone size={18} className="text-purple-400" /> : <Users size={18} className="text-blue-400" />}
              </div>
            )}
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">{res.name}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase">{res.type}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        {!isSearching && searchResults.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center mt-10 px-4">
            <p className="text-black dark:text-white/60 mb-2">No results found.</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/40 mb-4">
              Nostr search can be slow for new accounts.
              <br />
              For a 100% guaranteed connection, ask your friend to send you their <strong>npub</strong> or <strong>NIP-05 address</strong> (like name@domain.com) and paste it here.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/chat/ForwardModal.jsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { X, Search, Users, User, Megaphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function ForwardModal({ isOpen, onClose, onForward }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const dms = useChatStore((s) => s.dms);
  const groups = useChatStore((s) => s.groups);
  const profiles = useChatStore((s) => s.profiles);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Build unified list of chats
  const dmList = Object.keys(dms).map(pk => ({
    id: pk,
    isGroup: false,
    name: profiles[pk]?.displayName || profiles[pk]?.name || `User ${pk.slice(0, 6)}`,
    icon: User
  }));

  const groupList = groups.map(g => ({
    id: g.id,
    isGroup: true,
    name: g.name,
    isChannel: g.isChannel,
    icon: g.isChannel ? Megaphone : Users
  }));

  const allChats = [...dmList, ...groupList].filter(c => 
    c.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
  );

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/10 rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md h-[70vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl animate-slide-up">
        
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/10 flex justify-between items-center bg-[#0a0a0a]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Forward to...</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 text-white/50 hover:text-white bg-white/5 rounded-full">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3 border-b border-white/5 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
          <div className="flex items-center bg-black border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">
            <Search size={16} className="text-white/40 mr-2" />
            <input 
              type="text" 
              placeholder="Search chats and groups..." 
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-transparent text-sm text-white outline-none w-full"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2">
          {allChats.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-center text-white/40 text-sm mt-10">No chats found.</p>
          )}
          {allChats.map((chat) => {
            const Icon = chat.icon;
            return (
              <div 
                key={chat.id}
                onClick={() => onForward(chat.id, chat.isGroup)}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 hover:bg-white/5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors"
              >
                <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${chat.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
                  <Icon size={20} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{chat.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase">{chat.isGroup ? (chat.isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Group') : 'Direct Message'}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

FILE: src/components/chat/ReviewModal.jsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Star } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ReviewModal({ targetName, onSubmit, onClose }) {
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(0);
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(0);
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (rating < 1) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    const ok = await onSubmit(rating, text.trim());
    setSubmitting(false);
    if (ok) onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 relative shadow-2xl">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-black dark:text-white/50 hover:text-black dark:text-white"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white mb-1">Rate {targetName}</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white/50 mb-4">
          Your review is published to the Nostr network. Vendors can't delete it.
        </p>

        {/* Star picker */}
        <div className="flex justify-center gap-2 mb-6">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
            <button
              key={n}
              onClick={() => setRating(n)}
              onMouseEnter={() => setHover(n)}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHover(0)}
              className="transition-transform hover:scale-110 active:scale-95"
            >
              <Star
                size={36}
                className={
                  n <= (hover || rating)
                    ? 'fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400'
                    : 'text-black dark:text-white/20'
                }
              />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <textarea
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="How was your experience? (optional)"
          maxLength={500}
          rows={4}
          className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm text-black dark:text-white placeholder-white/30 focus:border-white/30 outline-none resize-none mb-2"
        />
        <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/30 text-right mb-4">
          {text.length}/500
        </p>

        <button
          onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={rating < 1 || submitting}
          className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-black dark:text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl transition-colors"
        >
          {submitting ? 'Publishing...' : rating < 1 ? 'Select a rating' : 'Publish Review'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Plus, X, Users, Megaphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

function MessageIcon({ size }) {
  return <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><path d="M21 15a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7l-4 4V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h14a2 2 0 0 1 2 2z"></path></svg>;
}

export default function FloatingMenu() {
  const showMenu = useChatStore((s) => s.showMenu);
  const setShowMenu = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowMenu);
  const setView = useChatStore((s) => s.setView);
  const setShowCreateGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowCreateGroup);
  const setIsChannel = useChatStore((s) => s.setIsChannel);

  return (
    <>
      {showMenu && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setShowMenu(false)}>
          <div className="absolute bottom-[10rem] right-6 flex flex-col gap-3 items-end">
            <button
              onClick={() => { setShowMenu(false); setView('search'); }}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-[#1c1c1e] px-4 py-3 rounded-full border border-white/10 shadow-2xl animate-slide-up"
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">New Chat</span>
              <div className="p-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full text-white"><MessageIcon size={18} /></div>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => { setShowMenu(false); setIsChannel(false); setShowCreateGroup(true); }}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-[#1c1c1e] px-4 py-3 rounded-full border border-white/10 shadow-2xl animate-slide-up"
              style={{ animationDelay: '50ms' }}
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">New Group</span>
              <div className="p-2 bg-green-500 rounded-full text-white"><Users size={18} /></div>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => { setShowMenu(false); setIsChannel(true); setShowCreateGroup(true); }}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-[#1c1c1e] px-4 py-3 rounded-full border border-white/10 shadow-2xl animate-slide-up"
              style={{ animationDelay: '100ms' }}
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">New Channel</span>
              <div className="p-2 bg-purple-500 rounded-full text-white"><Megaphone size={18} /></div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <button
        onClick={() => setShowMenu(!showMenu)}
        className={`fixed bottom-24 right-6 p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg transition-all z-[70] ${
          showMenu ? 'bg-[#1c1c1e] border border-white/20 text-white rotate-45' : 'bg-white text-black hover:scale-105 active:scale-90'
        }`}
      >
        <Plus size={24} className={showMenu ? 'rotate-45 transition-transform' : 'transition-transform'} />
      </button>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/chat/ChatList.jsx

import React from 'react';
import { Users, Megaphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function ChatList({ onOpenChat }) {
  const dms = useChatStore((s) => s.dms);
  const groups = useChatStore((s) => s.groups);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.groupMessages);
  const profiles = useChatStore((s) => s.profiles);
  const unreadCounts = useChatStore((s) => s.unreadCounts);

  // Compute DM list (with fallback for missing profiles)
  const dmList = Object.entries(dms).map(([pubkey, msgs]) => {
    const profile = profiles[pubkey];
    const safeProfile = (profile && profile.name && !/^[0-9a-f]{8,64}$/i.test(profile.name))
      ? profile
      : { displayName: `User ${pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`, picture: null, name: pubkey.slice(0, 4) };
    return { type: 'dm', id: pubkey, lastMsg: msgs[msgs.length - 1], profile: safeProfile };
  });

  // Compute group list
  const groupList = groups.map(g => ({
    type: 'group',
    id: g.id,
    group: g,
    lastMsg: groupMessages[g.id]?.[groupMessages[g.id].length - 1]
  }));

  // Deduplicate by chat ID to prevent React "duplicate key" errors
  const chatMap = new Map();
  [...dmList, ...groupList].forEach(c => chatMap.set(c.id, c));
  
  const allChats = Array.from(chatMap.values()).sort(
    (a, b) => (b.lastMsg?.timestamp || 0) - (a.lastMsg?.timestamp || 0)
  );

  if (allChats.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-center text-black dark:text-white/40 mt-10">No chats yet.</p>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      {allChats.map((chat) => {
        if (chat.type === 'dm') {
          const profile = profiles[chat.id] || { displayName: `User ${chat.id.slice(0, 4)}`, picture: null };
          return (
            <div
              key={chat.id}
              onClick={() => onOpenChat('dm', chat.id)}
              className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-4"
            >
              {profile.picture ? (
                <img src={profile.picture} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                  {profile.displayName.charAt(0)}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">{profile.displayName}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 truncate">
                  {chat.lastMsg?.data?.type === 'cipher_order' ? '📦 Order'
                    : chat.lastMsg?.data?.type === 'image' ? '🖼️ Photo'
                    : chat.lastMsg?.data?.type === 'audio' ? '🎤 Voice Note'
                    : chat.lastMsg?.data?.message || ''}
                </p>
              </div>
              {unreadCounts[chat.id] > 0 && (
                <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-blue-500 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-black dark:text-white shrink-0">
                  {unreadCounts[chat.id]}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        } else {
          const Icon = chat.group.isChannel ? Megaphone : Users;
          return (
            <div
              key={chat.id}
              onClick={() => onOpenChat('group', chat.id, chat.group)}
              className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-4"
            >
              <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${chat.group.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
                <Icon size={20} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">
                  {chat.group.name && !chat.group.name.startsWith('Group ') ? chat.group.name : 'Unnamed Group'}
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 truncate">
                  {chat.group.isChannel ? '📢 Channel' : '👥 Public Group'}
                </p>
              </div>
              {unreadCounts[chat.id] > 0 && (
                <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-blue-500 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-black dark:text-white shrink-0">
                  {unreadCounts[chat.id]}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        }
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/chat/ChatHeader.jsx

import React from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Megaphone, Users, Info, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

const copyToClipboard = async (text) => {
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      return true;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
    ta.value = text;
    ta.style.position = 'fixed';
    ta.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(ta);
    ta.select();
    document.execCommand('copy');
    document.body.removeChild(ta);
    return true;
  } catch (e) { return false; }
};

export default function ChatHeader({ mode }) {
  const activeDm = useChatStore((s) => s.activeDm);
  const activeGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.activeGroup);
  const profiles = useChatStore((s) => s.profiles);
  const typingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.typingUsers);
  const groupTypingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.groupTypingUsers);
  const setView = useChatStore((s) => s.setView);
  const setProfilePubkey = useChatStore((s) => s.setProfilePubkey);
  const setShowGroupInfo = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowGroupInfo);
  const setToast = useChatStore((s) => s.setToast);

  const handleBack = () => setView('list');

  const handleCopy = async (text, successMsg) => {
    const ok = await copyToClipboard(text);
    if (ok) setToast({ type: 'success', message: successMsg });
  };

  if (mode === 'dm' && activeDm) {
    const profile = profiles[activeDm] || { displayName: `User ${activeDm.slice(0, 4)}`, picture: null };
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a]/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 flex-shrink-0">
        <button onClick={handleBack} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white active:scale-90 transition-transform">
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity" onClick={() => setProfilePubkey(activeDm)}>
          {profile.picture ? (
            <img src={profile.picture} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full" />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">
              {profile.displayName.charAt(0)}
            </div>
          )}
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">{profile.displayName}</h3>
            {typingUsers[activeDm] ? (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-blue-400 animate-pulse">typing...</p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-black dark:text-white/40">Direct Message</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (mode === 'group' && activeGroup) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a]/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 flex-shrink-0">
        <button onClick={handleBack} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white active:scale-90 transition-transform">
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${activeGroup.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
          {activeGroup.isChannel ? <Megaphone size={20} /> : <Users size={20} />}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">{activeGroup.name}</h3>
          {Object.keys(groupTypingUsers).length > 0 ? (
            <p className="text-[11px] text-blue-400 animate-pulse truncate">
              {profiles[Object.keys(groupTypingUsers)[0]]?.displayName || `User ${Object.keys(groupTypingUsers)[0].slice(0, 4)}`} is typing...
            </p>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-[11px] text-black dark:text-white/40 flex items-center gap-2">
              {activeGroup.isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Group'}
              <span
                className="text-[10px] bg-white/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-black dark:text-white/50 cursor-pointer hover:text-black dark:text-white"
                onClick={() => handleCopy(activeGroup.id, 'Group ID Copied')}
              >
                ID: {activeGroup.id.slice(0, 8)}...
              </span>
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setShowGroupInfo(true)} className="p-2.5 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/70 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors">
          <Info size={18} />
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => handleCopy(
            `Join my group "${activeGroup.name}" on Cipher!\nLink: ${window.location.origin}?group=${activeGroup.id}&name=${encodeURIComponent(activeGroup.name || 'Unnamed Group')}`,
            'Invite Link Copied!'
          )}
          className="p-2.5 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/70 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <Share2 size={18} />
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return null;
}

=========================================
FILE: src/components/chat/VendorDashboard.jsx
=========================================

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, Package, DollarSign, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { useVendorStats } from '../../hooks/useVendorStats';
import OrderManagerModal from './OrderManagerModal';
import { useNIP44DM } from '../../hooks/useNIP44DM';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { db } from '../../db';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../../lib/relays';

export default function VendorDashboard({ identity, products, onBack }) {
  const { dms } = useChatStore();
  const { stats, loading, fetchStats } = useVendorStats({ identity });
  const { sendDM } = useNIP44DM({ identity });
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);
  const [selectedOrder, setSelectedOrder] = useState(null);
  const [fetchingOrders, setFetchingOrders] = useState(false);

  // Fetch orders from BOTH relay + local Dexie (union, deduplicated by order id)
  const loadOrders = async () => {
    setFetchingOrders(true);
    try {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');

      // 1. Get local overrides from Dexie (source of truth for local status changes)
      const localMsgs = await db.messages.toArray();
      const localMap = new Map();
      localMsgs.forEach(m => {
        if (m.data?.type === 'cipher_order') localMap.set(m.id, m);
      });

      // 2. Fetch DMs from Relay
      const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk], limit: 100 }, 5000);
      
      const combinedMap = new Map();

      // Add relay events, but merge local status if it exists (so it doesn't overwrite your 'shipped' click)
      for (const ev of events) {
        try {
          const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, ev.pubkey);
          const data = JSON.parse(nip44.v2.decrypt(ev.content, shared));
          
          if (data.type === 'cipher_order') {
            const localVersion = localMap.get(ev.id);
            const finalStatus = localVersion?.data?.status || data.status;
            
            combinedMap.set(ev.id, { 
              id: ev.id, chatId: ev.pubkey, pubkey: ev.pubkey, timestamp: ev.created_at, 
              data: { ...data, status: finalStatus } 
            });
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      }

      // Add any local orders that weren't in the relay fetch
      localMsgs.forEach(m => {
        if (m.data?.type === 'cipher_order' && !combinedMap.has(m.id)) {
          combinedMap.set(m.id, m);
        }
      });

      const combined = Array.from(combinedMap.values());
      
      // Sort: Pending first, Completed last
      combined.sort((a, b) => {
        const aComp = a.data.status === 'completed' ? 1 : 0;
        const bComp = b.data.status === 'completed' ? 1 : 0;
        return aComp - bComp;
      });

      setOrders(combined);
    } finally {
      setFetchingOrders(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchStats();
    loadOrders();
  }, [dms, fetchStats]);

  const handleUpdateStatus = async (order, newStatus) => {
    try {
      const updatedMsg = { ...order, data: { ...order.data, status: newStatus } };
      if (updatedMsg.chatId) {
        await db.messages.put(updatedMsg);
      }

      const payload = {
        type: 'order_update',
        orderId: order.id,
        productName: order.data.productName,
        status: newStatus
      };
      await sendDM(order.chatId, payload, null, null);

      setOrders(prev => prev.map(o => o.id === order.id ? updatedMsg : o));
      useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Order status updated to: ' + newStatus.toUpperCase() });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to update status', e);
      useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'info', message: 'Failed to update status: ' + e.message });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-white dark:bg-black relative">
      {/* Fixed Header Area */}
      <div className="flex-shrink-0 p-4 pb-0">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={onBack} className="p-2 text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white">
              <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2"><path d="M19 12H5M12 19l-7-7 7-7"/></svg>
            </button>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white">Vendor Dashboard</h2>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => { fetchStats(); loadOrders(); }}
            className="p-2 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={18} className={(loading || fetchingOrders) ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-6">
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1"><BarChart3 size={14}/> Total Sales</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white">{stats.sales}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1"><DollarSign size={14}/> Fiat Volume</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-400">${stats.revenueFiat.toFixed(2)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-black dark:text-white/80 mb-3">Orders ({orders.length})</h3>
      </div>

      {/* Scrollable Orders Area */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 px-4 pb-24">
        {fetchingOrders ? (
          <p className="text-black dark:text-white/50 text-center py-10">Fetching orders from relay...</p>
        ) : orders.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-black dark:text-white/50 text-center py-10">No orders yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {orders.map(o => (
              <div 
                key={o.id} 
                onClick={() => setSelectedOrder(o)}
                className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-4 cursor-pointer hover:border-white/30 transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-bold text-black dark:text-white text-sm">{o.data.productName}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50">SHIP TO: {o.data.shippingAddress}</p>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-green-400 font-bold">${o.data.amountFiat}</p>
                </div>
                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full border ${
                  o.data.status === 'completed' ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-400 border-green-500/20' : 'bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-400 border-yellow-500/20'
                }`}>
                  {o.data.status?.toUpperCase() || 'PENDING'}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {selectedOrder && (
        <OrderManagerModal
          order={selectedOrder}
          onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
          onClose={() => setSelectedOrder(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/chat/ChatPinnedBar.jsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Pin, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function ChatPinnedBar({ unpinMessage }) {
  const activeGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.activeGroup);
  const pinnedMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.pinnedMessages);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.groupMessages);

  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);

  if (!activeGroup) return null;
  const pinnedId = pinnedMessages[activeGroup.id];
  if (!pinnedId) return null;

  const groupMsgs = groupMessages[activeGroup.id] || [];
  const pinnedMsg = groupMsgs.find((m) => m.id === pinnedId);
  if (!pinnedMsg) return null;

  let preview = 'Pinned message';
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(pinnedMsg.rawContent || '{}');
    preview = parsed.message || parsed.url || 'Pinned message';
  } catch (e) {
    preview = pinnedMsg.rawContent || 'Pinned message';
  }

  return (
    <div className="border-b border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
      <div
        onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/[0.02] transition-colors"
      >
        <Pin size={14} className="text-yellow-400 shrink-0" />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <p className="text-[10px] text-yellow-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Pinned</p>
          <p className={`text-xs text-black dark:text-white/70 ${expanded ? '' : 'truncate'}`}>{preview}</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={async (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            if (unpinMessage) await unpinMessage(activeGroup.id);
          }}
          className="p-1 text-black dark:text-white/40 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <X size={14} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
