import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { Reply, Copy, Forward, Pin, Trash2, ShieldAlert, Bookmark, Pencil, X } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MessageActionsSheet({
  isOpen, onClose, onAction, msg, isAdmin, isMine, canEdit
}) {
  const [canClose, setCanClose] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      const t = setTimeout(() => setCanClose(true), 150);
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    } else {
      setCanClose(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen || !msg) return null;

  const emojis = ['❤️', '👍', '🔥', '😂', '😮', '😢', '🙏'];

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-[2px] flex items-center justify-center p-4" onClick={() => canClose && onClose()}>
      <div
        className="bg-[#1c1c1e] w-[300px] rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-white/10 overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-100"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Emojis */}
        <div className="flex justify-around p-2 border-b border-white/10 bg-[#2c2c2e]">
          {emojis.map(emoji => (
            <button
              key={emoji}
              onClick={() => { onAction('react', emoji); onClose(); }}
              className="w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center text-xl hover:scale-125 transition-transform"
            >
              {emoji}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Actions Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 p-2">
          <button onClick={() => { onAction('reply'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
            <Reply size={20} className="text-blue-400" />
            <span className="text-[10px] text-white/80 font-semibold">Reply</span>
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => { onAction('copy'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
            <Copy size={20} className="text-white/70" />
            <span className="text-[10px] text-white/80 font-semibold">Copy</span>
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => { onAction('forward'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
            <Forward size={20} className="text-white/70" />
            <span className="text-[10px] text-white/80 font-semibold">Forward</span>
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => { onAction('save_to_self'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
            <Bookmark size={20} className="text-green-400" />
            <span className="text-[10px] text-white/80 font-semibold">Save</span>
          </button>

          {canEdit && (
            <button onClick={() => { onAction('edit'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
              <Pencil size={20} className="text-white/70" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-white/80 font-semibold">Edit</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {isAdmin && (
            <button onClick={() => { onAction('pin'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
              <Pin size={20} className="text-yellow-400" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-yellow-400 font-semibold">Pin</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {isMine && (
            <button onClick={() => { onAction('delete'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
              <Trash2 size={20} className="text-red-400" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-red-400 font-semibold">Delete</span>
            </button>
          )}

          <button onClick={() => { onAction('report'); onClose(); }} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
            <ShieldAlert size={20} className="text-red-400" />
            <span className="text-[10px] text-red-400 font-semibold">Report</span>

    document.body

FILE: src/components/MessageBubble.jsx

import React from 'react';
import { Package, CheckCircle2, Forward } from 'lucide-react';

function renderMessage(text) {
  if (!text) return null;
  const parts = text.split(/(\|\|.*?\|\|)/g);
  return parts.map((part, i) => {
    if (part.startsWith('||') && part.endsWith('||')) {
      return <span key={i} onClick={(e) => { e.currentTarget.classList.remove('blur-sm', 'text-transparent'); e.currentTarget.classList.add('text-white'); }} className="bg-surfaceLight px-1 rounded cursor-pointer blur-sm text-transparent transition-all select-none">{part.slice(2, -2)}</span>;
    }
    const boldParts = part.split(/(\*\*.*?\*\*)/g);
    return boldParts.map((bp, j) => {
      if (bp.startsWith('**') && bp.endsWith('**')) return <strong key={`${i}-${j}`}>{bp.slice(2, -2)}</strong>;
      return bp;
    });
  });
}

export default function MessageBubble({
  msg, data, showTail, repliedTo, repliedData, onSelect, isGroup,
  onSenderClick, senderName, reactions, onReact, myPubkey, isSelected, onAction
}) {
  const type = data?.type || 'text';
  const bodyText = data?.message || '';

  const aggregated = (() => {
    if (!reactions || reactions.length === 0) return [];
    const map = {};
    reactions.forEach((r) => {
      if (!map[r.emoji]) map[r.emoji] = { emoji: r.emoji, count: 0, hasMine: false };
      map[r.emoji].count++;
      if (r.pubkey === myPubkey) map[r.emoji].hasMine = true;
    });
    return Object.values(map);
  })();

  return (
    <div className={`relative flex flex-col ${msg.isSender ? 'items-end' : 'items-start'} animate-fade-in`}>
      <div
        onClick={() => onSelect?.(msg, data)}
        className={`max-w-[85%] px-4 py-2 relative shadow-sm cursor-pointer group ${
          msg.isSender
            ? `bg-[#2b5278] text-white ${showTail ? 'rounded-2xl rounded-tr-sm' : 'rounded-2xl'}`
            : `bg-[#182533] text-white ${showTail ? 'rounded-2xl rounded-tl-sm' : 'rounded-2xl'}`
        } ${isSelected ? 'ring-2 ring-blue-500/50' : ''}`}
      >
        {repliedTo && (
          <div className="bg-black/20 p-2 rounded-lg mb-2 border-l-2 border-blue-400 text-xs text-white/70 truncate">
            {repliedData?.message || repliedData?.url || 'Message'}
          </div>
        )}

        {!msg.isSender && isGroup && (
          <p onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onSenderClick && onSenderClick(msg.pubkey); }} className="text-[11px] text-blue-300 font-semibold mb-1 cursor-pointer hover:underline">
            {senderName || `User ${msg.pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`}
          </p>
        )}

        {type === 'cipher_order' && (
          <div className="bg-[#1a1a1a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3 mb-2 min-w-[240px] shadow-lg">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 pb-2 border-b border-white/10">
              <Package size={16} className="text-blue-400" />
              <span className="font-bold text-sm text-white">Order Receipt</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1 mb-2">
              <p className="text-[11px] text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider">Product</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white truncate">{data.productName || 'Unknown Product'}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-black/40 p-2 rounded-lg mb-2">
              <div>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/50 uppercase">Amount</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-green-400">${data.amountFiat ? Number(data.amountFiat).toFixed(2) : '0.00'}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/50 uppercase">Crypto</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-white/80">{data.amountCrypto} {data.currency?.toUpperCase()}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {data.shippingAddress && (
              <div className="bg-black/40 p-2 rounded-lg mb-2">
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/50 uppercase mb-1">Ship To</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/90 whitespace-pre-wrap">{data.shippingAddress}</p>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
              <div className="flex justify-center">
                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full border ${
                  data.status === 'completed' ? 'text-green-400 bg-green-400/10 border-green-400/20' :
                  data.status === 'shipped' ? 'text-purple-400 bg-purple-400/10 border-purple-400/20' :
                  data.status === 'accepted' ? 'text-blue-400 bg-blue-400/10 border-blue-400/20' :
                  'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-400/10 border-yellow-400/20'
                }`}>
                  {(data.status || 'payment_sent').replace('_', ' ').toUpperCase()}
                </span>
              </div>

              {msg.isSender && data.status !== 'completed' && onAction && (
                <button
                  onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onAction('confirm_delivery', msg); }}
                  className="w-full mt-1 bg-green-500 hover:bg-green-400 text-black text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wider py-3 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(34,197,94,0.4)] active:scale-95"
                >
                  Confirm Delivery
                </button>
              )}

              {!msg.isSender && data.status !== 'completed' && (
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 text-center italic">Waiting for buyer to confirm delivery...</p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {type === 'image' && (
          <div className="relative">
            <img src={data.url} className="rounded-lg max-h-60 object-cover mb-1" />
            {data.metadata_stripped && (
              <div className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 bg-black/70 text-[9px] text-green-400 font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded flex items-center gap-1 border border-green-500/30">
                🛡️ SAFE
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        {type === 'audio' && <audio controls src={data.url} className="max-w-full h-10 mb-1" />}
        {data.forwardedFrom && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-blue-300/80 italic mb-1">
            <Forward size={12} /> Forwarded from {data.forwardedFrom}
          </div>
        )}
        {type === 'text' && <p className="text-[15px] leading-snug break-words overflow-hidden w-full">{renderMessage(bodyText)}</p>}

        {type === 'order_update' && (
          <div className="bg-blue-600/20 border border-blue-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 mb-2 flex items-center gap-3">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} className="text-blue-400" />
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Order Update</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-blue-300">Vendor marked order as <strong>{data.status?.toUpperCase()}</strong></p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1 mt-1 opacity-60">
          <span className="text-[10px]">{new Date(msg.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {aggregated.length > 0 && (
        <div className={`flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-1 ${msg.isSender ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
          {aggregated.map((r) => (
            <button
              key={r.emoji}
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReact && onReact(r.emoji); }}
              className={`text-[11px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full transition-colors ${
                r.hasMine
                  ? 'bg-blue-500/30 border border-blue-400/60 text-white'
                  : 'bg-white/10 border border-white/10 text-white/80 hover:bg-white/20'
              }`}
            >
              {r.emoji} {r.count}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FILE: src/components/OneTapCheckout.jsx
=========================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { ReactQRCode } from '@lglab/react-qr-code';
import { X, Copy, CheckCircle2, ShieldCheck, DollarSign } from 'lucide-react';

const usePriceOracle = () => {
  const [prices, setPrices] = useState({ btc: 65000, xmr: 150 });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchPrices = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/simple/price?ids=bitcoin,monero&vs_currencies=usd');
        const data = await res.json();
        setPrices({ btc: data.bitcoin.usd, xmr: data.monero.usd });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Price oracle failed:", err);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchPrices();
  }, []);

  return { prices, loading };
};

export default function OneTapCheckout({ isOpen, onClose, product, vendor, identity, onConfirmOrder }) {
  const { prices, loading } = usePriceOracle();
  const [selectedCurrency, setSelectedCurrency] = useState('btc');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [shippingAddress, setShippingAddress] = useState('');

  if (!isOpen || !product) return null;

  const usdPrice = product?.priceUsd || 29.99;
  const btcAmount = prices.btc > 0 ? (usdPrice / prices.btc).toFixed(8) : '0.00000000';
  const xmrAmount = prices.xmr > 0 ? (usdPrice / prices.xmr).toFixed(6) : '0.000000';

  const currentAddress = selectedCurrency === 'btc' ? (vendor?.btcAddress || 'bc1q...') : (vendor?.xmrAddress || '4...');
  const currentAmount = selectedCurrency === 'btc' ? btcAmount : xmrAmount;

  const paymentUri = selectedCurrency === 'btc'
    ? `bitcoin:${currentAddress}?amount=${currentAmount}&label=${encodeURIComponent(product.name)}&message=Order%20from%20Cipher`
    : `monero:${currentAddress}?tx_amount=${currentAmount}&tx_description=${encodeURIComponent(product.name)}`;

  const handleCopyAddress = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(currentAddress);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  // THIS IS THE FIX: We explicitly build the payload here before passing it up
  const handleConfirmPayment = () => {
    onConfirmOrder({
      productId: product?.id || 'unknown',
      productName: product?.name || 'Test Product',
      amountFiat: usdPrice,
      amountCrypto: currentAmount,
      currency: selectedCurrency,
      vendorPubkey: vendor?.pubkey || identity?.pk || '',
      shippingAddress: shippingAddress,
      paymentAddress: currentAddress,
    });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {isOpen && (
        <>
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} onClick={onClose} className="fixed inset-0 bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-[100]" />
          <motion.div initial={{ y: "100%" }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: "100%" }} transition={{ type: "spring", damping: 25, stiffness: 200 }} className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-[#0a0a0a] border-t border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-t-3xl z-[101] h-[90vh] max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
            <div className="w-12 h-1.5 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mt-3 mb-2" />
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1 min-h-0">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3 sm:mb-6">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white mb-1">{product.name}</h2>
                  <p className="text-black dark:text-white/60 text-sm flex items-center gap-1"><ShieldCheck size={14} className="text-green-400" /> Secure Checkout</p>
                </div>
                <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white"><X size={20} /></button>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white/5 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-4 mb-6">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                  <span className="text-black dark:text-white/60 text-sm">Total Price</span>
                  <span className="text-black dark:text-white font-bold text-lg flex items-center"><DollarSign size={16} className="text-black dark:text-white/60" /> {usdPrice.toFixed(2)} USD</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                  <span className="text-black dark:text-white/40">Crypto Equivalent</span>
                  <span className="text-black dark:text-white/80 font-mono">{loading ? "Calculating..." : selectedCurrency === 'btc' ? `${btcAmount} BTC` : `${xmrAmount} XMR`}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3 sm:mb-6">
                <button onClick={() => setSelectedCurrency('btc')} className={`flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl border font-semibold text-sm transition-all ${selectedCurrency === 'btc' ? 'bg-orange-500/20 border-orange-500 text-orange-400' : 'bg-white/5 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 text-black dark:text-white/60'}`}>Pay with BTC</button>
                <button onClick={() => setSelectedCurrency('xmr')} className={`flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl border font-semibold text-sm transition-all ${selectedCurrency === 'xmr' ? 'bg-orange-500/20 border-orange-500 text-orange-400' : 'bg-white/5 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 text-black dark:text-white/60'}`}>Pay with XMR</button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-white p-4 sm:p-6 rounded-2xl mb-3 sm:mb-6">
                {loading ? <div className="w-48 h-48 flex items-center justify-center text-black/50">Loading price...</div> : <ReactQRCode value={paymentUri} size={160} />}
                <p className="text-black/50 text-xs mt-4 text-center max-w-[200px] break-all">Scan with your wallet app</p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
                <div className="flex-1 bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 overflow-hidden">
                  <p className="text-black dark:text-white/40 text-xs mb-1">Vendor Address</p>
                  <p className="text-black dark:text-white text-sm font-mono truncate">{currentAddress}</p>
                </div>
                <button onClick={handleCopyAddress} className="p-3 bg-white/10 rounded-xl text-black dark:text-white/80 hover:bg-borderDark transition-colors">{copied ? <CheckCircle2 size={20} className="text-green-400" /> : <Copy size={20} />}</button>
              </div>

              <div className="mb-4">
                <label className="block text-xs text-black dark:text-white/60 mb-1">Shipping Address / Delivery Details</label>
                <textarea 
                  value={shippingAddress}
                  onChange={(e) => setShippingAddress(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Enter your address or pickup details..."
                  rows={2}
                  style={{ width: '100%', minHeight: '60px' }}
                  className="bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm text-black dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500 resize-none box-border"
                />
              </div>

              <button disabled={!shippingAddress.trim()} onClick={handleConfirmPayment} className="w-full bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-black dark:text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                I've Sent the Payment
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        </>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=========================================
FILE: src/components/Calculator.jsx
=========================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';

export default function Calculator({ onUnlock }) {
  const [display, setDisplay] = useState('0');
  const [pinAttempt, setPinAttempt] = useState('');

  const handlePress = async (val) => {
    if (val === 'AC') {
      setDisplay('0');
      setPinAttempt('');
    } else if (val === '=') {
      // Extract just the numbers from whatever they typed
      const pin = display.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
      
      // If they typed 4 or more numbers, try to unlock the vault
      if (pin.length >= 4) {
        const success = await onUnlock(pin);
        if (success) return; // If successful, the app will unmount the calculator
      }

      // If unlock fails (or they were doing normal math), calculate it
      try {
        // eslint-disable-next-line no-eval
        const result = eval(display);
        setDisplay(String(result));
      } catch {
        setDisplay('Error');
      }
      setPinAttempt('');
    } else {
      setPinAttempt(prev => prev + val);
      setDisplay(prev => (prev === '0' || prev === 'Error') ? val : prev + val);
    }
  };

  const buttons = [
    'AC', '+/-', '%', '÷',
    '7', '8', '9', '×',
    '4', '5', '6', '-',
    '1', '2', '3', '+',
    '0', '.', '='
  ];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white dark:bg-black flex flex-col justify-end p-4 pb-12 z-[9999]">
      <div className="flex-1 flex items-end justify-end p-6 mb-4">
        <h1 className="text-black dark:text-white text-7xl font-light tracking-tighter truncate max-w-full">
          {display}
        </h1>
      </div>
      
      <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        {buttons.map((btn) => {
          const isZero = btn === '0';
          const isOperator = ['÷', '×', '-', '+', '='].includes(btn);
          const isTopRow = ['AC', '+/-', '%'].includes(btn);
          
          return (
            <button
              key={btn}
              onClick={() => handlePress(btn)}
              className={`
                ${isZero ? 'col-span-2 justify-start pl-8' : ''}
                ${isOperator ? 'bg-[#ff9f0a] text-black dark:text-white' : isTopRow ? 'bg-[#a5a5a5] text-black' : 'bg-[#333333] text-black dark:text-white'}
                h-20 rounded-full text-3xl font-medium flex items-center justify-center active:opacity-70 transition-opacity
              `}
            >
              {btn}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';

export default function LockScreen({ onUnlock }) {
  const [display, setDisplay] = useState('0');
  const [pinAttempt, setPinAttempt] = useState('');

  const handlePress = (val) => {
    if (val === 'AC') {
      setDisplay('0');
      setPinAttempt('');
    } else if (val === '=') {
      // 1234 is the secret PIN to unlock
      if (pinAttempt === '1234' || display === '1234') {
        onUnlock();
      } else {
        try {
          // Act like a real calculator if it's not the PIN
          // eslint-disable-next-line no-eval
          const result = eval(display);
          setDisplay(String(result));
        } catch {
          setDisplay('Error');
        }
        setPinAttempt('');
      }
    } else {
      setPinAttempt(prev => prev + val);
      setDisplay(prev => (prev === '0' || prev === 'Error') ? val : prev + val);
    }
  };

  const buttons = [
    'AC', '+/-', '%', '÷',
    '7', '8', '9', '×',
    '4', '5', '6', '-',
    '1', '2', '3', '+',
    '0', '.', '='
  ];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white dark:bg-black flex flex-col justify-end p-4 pb-12 z-[9999]">
      <div className="flex-1 flex items-end justify-end p-6 mb-4">
        <h1 className="text-black dark:text-white text-7xl font-light tracking-tighter truncate max-w-full">
          {display}
        </h1>
      </div>
      
      <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        {buttons.map((btn) => {
          const isZero = btn === '0';
          const isOperator = ['÷', '×', '-', '+', '='].includes(btn);
          const isTopRow = ['AC', '+/-', '%'].includes(btn);
          
          return (
            <button
              key={btn}
              onClick={() => handlePress(btn)}
              className={`
                ${isZero ? 'col-span-2 justify-start pl-8' : ''}
                ${isOperator ? 'bg-[#ff9f0a] text-black dark:text-white' : isTopRow ? 'bg-[#a5a5a5] text-black' : 'bg-[#333333] text-black dark:text-white'}
                h-20 rounded-full text-3xl font-medium flex items-center justify-center active:opacity-70 transition-opacity
              `}
            >
              {btn}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Store, ArrowLeft, Image as ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import OneTapCheckout from './OneTapCheckout';

export default function ShopView({ myProducts, setMyProducts, setCheckoutProduct, setView, identity, setToast, checkoutProduct, onConfirmOrder }) {
  const [showAddProduct, setShowAddProduct] = useState(false);
  const [newProduct, setNewProduct] = useState({ name: '', priceUsd: '', image: '' });

  return (
    <>
      <div className="p-4 h-full relative pb-24 bg-white dark:bg-black overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={() => setView('list')} className="p-2 text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white"><ArrowLeft size={20}/></button>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">My Shop</h2>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setShowAddProduct(true)} className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-black dark:text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition-colors">
            + Add Product
          </button>
        </div>

        {myProducts.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center text-black dark:text-white/40 mt-20">
            <Store size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 opacity-50" />
            <p>No products yet.</p>
            <p className="text-sm mt-2">Add your first product to start selling.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            {myProducts.map(p => (
              <div key={p.id} className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col group">
                <div className="relative h-32 w-full cursor-pointer overflow-hidden" onClick={() => setCheckoutProduct(p)}>
                  {p.image ? (
                    <img src={p.image} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full bg-white/5 flex items-center justify-center text-black dark:text-white/20"><ImageIcon size={32} /></div>
                  )}
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white dark:bg-black/40 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity">
                    <span className="text-black dark:text-white text-xs font-bold bg-white dark:bg-black/60 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full">View</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="p-3 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold truncate text-black dark:text-white">{p.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-green-400 font-bold">${p.priceUsd}</p>
                  </div>
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setCheckoutProduct(p)}
                    className="w-full mt-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-blue-600 text-black dark:text-white text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 hover:border-blue-500"
                  >
                    Buy Now
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {showAddProduct && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
            <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 relative">
              <button onClick={() => setShowAddProduct(false)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-black dark:text-white/50 hover:text-black dark:text-white">✕</button>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Add New Product</h2>
              <form onSubmit={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (!newProduct.name || !newProduct.priceUsd) return;
                const productId = Date.now().toString();
                const newProdObj = { ...newProduct, id: productId, priceUsd: parseFloat(newProduct.priceUsd) };
                setMyProducts([...myProducts, newProdObj]);
                
                // Publish to Nostr relays as a NIP-99 Product Listing (kind 30402)
                try {
                  import('nostr-tools').then(async ({ finalizeEvent, SimplePool }) => {
                    const pool = new SimplePool();
                    const ev = finalizeEvent({
                      kind: 30402,
                      created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
                      tags: [
                        ['d', productId],
                        ['title', newProduct.name],
                        ['price', newProduct.priceUsd, 'USD'],
                        ['image', newProduct.image || ''],
                        ['t', 'cipher_marketplace'], // Filter tag
                        ['published_at', Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000).toString()]
                      ],
                      content: ''
                    }, identity.sk);
                    await pool.publish(['wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://groups.aegispayhq.com'], ev);
                    console.log('✅ Product published to relays!');
                  });
                } catch (e) { console.error('Failed to publish product:', e); }

                setNewProduct({ name: '', priceUsd: '', image: '' });
                setShowAddProduct(false);
                setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Product published globally!' });
              }} className="space-y-4">
                <input type="text" placeholder="Product Name" value={newProduct.name} onChange={e => setNewProduct({...newProduct, name: e.target.value})} className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 text-black dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500" required />
                <input type="number" step="0.01" placeholder="Price (USD)" value={newProduct.priceUsd} onChange={e => setNewProduct({...newProduct, priceUsd: e.target.value})} className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 text-black dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500" required />
                <input type="text" placeholder="Image URL (Optional)" value={newProduct.image} onChange={e => setNewProduct({...newProduct, image: e.target.value})} className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 text-black dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500" />
                <button type="submit" className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-black dark:text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg">Save Product</button>
              </form>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <OneTapCheckout
        isOpen={!!checkoutProduct}
        onClose={() => setCheckoutProduct(null)}
        product={checkoutProduct}
        vendor={{ pubkey: checkoutProduct?.pubkey || identity?.pk || '', btcAddress: 'bc1qxy2kgdygjrsqtzq2n0yrf2493p83kkfjhx0wlh', xmrAddress: '44AFFq5kSiGBoZ4NMDwYtN18obc8AemS33DBLWs3H7otXft3XjrpDtQGv7SqSsaBYBb98uNbr2VBBEt7f2wfn3RVGQBEP3A' }}
        identity={identity}
        onConfirmOrder={onConfirmOrder}
      />
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ProfileModal.jsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { X, MessageSquare, Shield, Star, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { useReputation } from '../hooks/useReputation';
import { useVendorStats } from '../hooks/useVendorStats';

export default function ProfileModal({ pubkey, profile, identity, onClose, onMessage, onReview }) {
  const { reputation, loading, fetchReputation, sendVouch } = useReputation({ identity });
  const { stats: vendorStats } = useVendorStats({ identity, targetPubkey: pubkey });
  const [vouching, setVouching] = useState(false);
  const [showFiltered, setShowFiltered] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pubkey) fetchReputation(pubkey);
  }, [pubkey, fetchReputation]);

  if (!pubkey) return null;

  const p = profile || {};
  const displayName = p.displayName || p.name || `User ${pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`;
  const picture = p.picture;
  const about = p.about || 'No bio yet.';
  const nip05 = p.nip05 || p.name;

  // Trust badges only count vouches that passed the age gate
  const isTrusted = reputation?.vouchCount >= 3;
  const isSeller = reputation?.reviewCount >= 5;
  const rating = reputation?.avgRating || 0;

  const isSelf = identity?.pk === pubkey;
  const isVouched = reputation?.hasVouched;

  const handleVouch = async () => {
    if (!identity?.pk || isSelf) return;
    setVouching(true);
    await sendVouch(pubkey);
    setVouching(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fade-in" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden shadow-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto animate-slide-up" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <button onClick={onClose} className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-10 p-2 bg-white dark:bg-black/50 rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/70 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors">
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <div className="w-full flex justify-center pt-3 pb-1 absolute top-0 z-20"><div className="w-12 h-1.5 bg-white/20 rounded-full"></div></div>
        <div className="h-32 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600/40 to-purple-600/40" />

        <div className="px-6 pb-6 relative">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-end -mt-12 mb-4">
            <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full border-4 border-[#111] bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#0a0a0a] flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden shadow-lg">
              {picture ? (
                <img src={picture} alt="avatar" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white/50">{displayName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => { onMessage(pubkey); onClose(); }}
                className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-black dark:text-white font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors shadow-lg"
              >
                <MessageSquare size={16} /> Message
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white">{displayName}</h2>
            {isTrusted && <Shield size={20} className="text-green-400 fill-green-400/20" title="Trusted (3+ vouches)" />}
            {isSeller && <Star size={20} className="text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400/20" title="Top Seller (5+ reviews)" />}
          </div>

          {/* Vendor Badges */}
          {vendorStats?.badges?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mb-3">
              {vendorStats.badges.map(b => (
                <div key={b.id} className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-white/5 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${b.color}`}>
                  <span>{b.icon}</span> {b.name}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {nip05 && <p className="text-sm text-blue-400 mb-3">@{nip05}</p>}

          {reputation && (
            <div className="flex gap-4 mb-3">
              <div className="flex flex-col">
                <span className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase">Trusted Vouches</span>
                <span className="text-lg font-bold text-black dark:text-white">{reputation.vouchCount}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col">
                <span className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase">Reviews</span>
                <span className="text-lg font-bold text-black dark:text-white">{reputation.reviewCount}</span>
              </div>
              {reputation.reviewCount > 0 && (
                <div className="flex flex-col">
                  <span className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase">Rating</span>
                  <span className="text-lg font-bold text-yellow-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                    {rating} <Star size={14} className="fill-yellow-400" />
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Show who vouched */}
          {reputation?.vouchList?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-3">
              <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Vouched by</p>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                {reputation.vouchList.slice(0, 5).map((v) => (
                  <span key={v.pubkey} className="text-[11px] bg-green-500/10 text-green-300 border border-green-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    @{v.name}
                  </span>
                ))}
                {reputation.vouchList.length > 5 && (
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-black dark:text-white/40">+{reputation.vouchList.length - 5} more</span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Show filtered vouches (transparency) */}
          {reputation?.filteredCount > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-3">
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowFiltered(!showFiltered)}
                className="text-[10px] text-yellow-500/70 hover:text-yellow-400 flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                <AlertTriangle size={10} />
                {reputation.filteredCount} low-trust vouch{reputation.filteredCount > 1 ? 'es' : ''} hidden
                <ChevronDown size={10} className={showFiltered ? 'rotate-180' : ''} />
              </button>
              {showFiltered && (
                <div className="mt-1 space-y-1 pl-3 border-l border-yellow-500/20">
                  {reputation.filteredList.map((v) => (
                    <p key={v.pubkey} className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40">
                      @{v.name} — {v.reason === 'too-new' ? `account only ${v.ageDays}d old` : v.reason}
                    </p>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-black/30 rounded-xl p-4 mt-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50">
            <p className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white/80 whitespace-pre-wrap">{about}</p>
          </div>

          {/* Vouch button — hidden on self-profile */}
          {!isSelf && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <button
                onClick={handleVouch}
                disabled={isVouched || vouching}
                className={`w-full font-bold py-2 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border ${
                  isVouched
                    ? 'bg-green-600/20 text-green-400 border-green-500/30'
                    : 'bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/30'
                } disabled:opacity-50`}
              >
                {isVouched ? (
                  <><CheckCircle2 size={16} /> Vouched</>
                ) : (
                  <><Shield size={16} /> {vouching ? 'Vouching...' : 'Vouch for Trust'}</>
                )}
              </button>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/30 text-center mt-1">
                Only counts if your account is 7+ days old
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {isSelf && (
            <div className="mt-4 p-3 bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 text-center">
              <p className="text-[11px] text-black dark:text-white/50">This is your profile</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!isSelf && onReview && (
            <button
              onClick={() => { onReview(pubkey); onClose(); }}
              className="w-full mt-2 bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-400 border border-yellow-500/30 font-bold py-2 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-yellow-500/20 transition-colors"
            >
              <Star size={16} /> Leave a Review
            </button>
          )}

          {reputation?.recentReviews?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-6">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-black dark:text-white mb-2">Recent Reviews</h3>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {reputation.recentReviews.map((rev, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-white/5 p-3 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="flex gap-1 mb-1">
                      {[...Array(5)].map((_, j) => (
                        <Star key={j} size={12} className={j < rev.rating ? 'text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400' : 'text-black dark:text-white/20'} />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/70">{rev.text}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Pubkey</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-black dark:text-white/70 truncate flex-1">{pubkey}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/MessageInput.jsx

import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import { Send, Image as ImageIcon, Mic, Square, Smile } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MessageInput({ 
  value, onChange, onSend, canPost, isChannel,
  onImageUpload, startRecording, stopRecording, isRecording, uploadingImage, onEmojiClick
}) {
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

  return (
    <div className="p-3 bg-[#111] border-t border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-2">
      {canPost ? (
        <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
          {/* Attach Image Button */}
          <button 
            type="button"
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            disabled={uploadingImage || isRecording}
            className="p-3 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] hover:bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/70 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <ImageIcon size={18} />
          </button>
          <input 
            type="file" 
            accept="image/*" 
            className="hidden" 
            ref={fileInputRef} 
            onChange={onImageUpload} 
          />

          {/* Voice Record Button */}
          <button 
            type="button"
            onMouseDown={startRecording}
            onMouseUp={stopRecording}
            onTouchStart={startRecording}
            onTouchEnd={stopRecording}
            disabled={uploadingImage}
            className={`p-3 rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/70 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 ${isRecording ? 'bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 animate-pulse' : 'bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] hover:bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717]'}`}
          >
            {isRecording ? <Square size={18} /> : <Mic size={18} />}
          </button>

          {/* Text Input */}
          <form
            onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onSend?.() }}
            className="flex-1 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] rounded-2xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 flex items-end p-1"
          >
            <textarea
              value={value}
              onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={isChannel ? "Broadcast..." : "Message"}
              className="w-full bg-transparent text-black dark:text-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none resize-none max-h-32"
              rows={1}
            />
          </form>

          {/* Send Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onSend}
            disabled={!value?.trim()}
            className="p-3 bg-blue-600 rounded-full text-black dark:text-white disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-blue-500 transition-colors"
          >
            <Send size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="text-center text-black dark:text-white/40 text-xs py-2">
          Only the channel creator can post here.
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/TutorialModal.jsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { MessageSquare, Compass, User, ShieldCheck, ChevronRight, X } from 'lucide-react';

const steps = [
  {
    icon: <ShieldCheck size={36} className="text-white relative z-10" />,
    title: "Welcome to Cipher",
    desc: "Your decentralized, encrypted messaging & commerce app. Let's take a quick tour of how to navigate."
  },
  {
    icon: <MessageSquare size={36} className="text-blue-400 relative z-10" />,
    title: "The Chats Tab",
    desc: "Tap 'Chats' at the bottom left to access your encrypted DMs. You can also create or join Groups here."
  },
  {
    icon: <Compass size={36} className="text-purple-400 relative z-10" />,
    title: "The Explore Tab",
    desc: "Tap 'Explore' in the middle to browse the Global Marketplace. Buy items using BTC or XMR seamlessly."
  },
  {
    icon: <User size={36} className="text-green-400 relative z-10" />,
    title: "The Profile Tab",
    desc: "Tap 'Profile' on the right. Manage your wallet, open your Vendor Dashboard, or edit your identity."
  }
];

export default function TutorialModal({ isOpen, onClose }) {
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(0);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (currentStep < steps.length - 1) {
      setCurrentStep(prev => prev + 1);
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('cipher_new_account');
      onClose();
    }
  };

  const step = steps[currentStep];

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div 
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }} 
        animate={{ opacity: 1 }} 
        exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md p-6"
      >
        <motion.div 
          key={currentStep}
          initial={{ x: 30, opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ x: -30, opacity: 0 }}
          transition={{ type: "spring", damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }}
          className="relative bg-[#0a0a0a]/90 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-white/10 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-sm p-8 overflow-hidden shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)]"
        >
          {/* Skip button */}
          <button 
            onClick={() => { localStorage.removeItem('cipher_new_account'); onClose(); }}
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>

          <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center mt-2">
            
            {/* Glowing Icon Box */}
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#1a1a1a] to-[#0a0a0a] rounded-[1.5rem] flex items-center justify-center mb-6 border border-white/10 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(59,130,246,0.15)] relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/5 blur-xl"></div>
              {step.icon}
            </div>
            
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-3">{step.title}</h2>
            <p className="text-[15px] text-gray-400 leading-relaxed mb-8 px-2">{step.desc}</p>

            {/* Sleek Progress Dots */}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-8">
              {steps.map((_, i) => (
                <div 
                  key={i} 
                  className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                    i === currentStep 
                      ? 'w-6 bg-blue-500 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(59,130,246,0.5)]' 
                      : 'w-1.5 bg-white/20'
                  }`}
                />
              ))}
            </div>

            <button 
              onClick={handleNext}
              className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-semibold text-[16px] tracking-wide py-3.5 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-[0_4px_20px_-4px_rgba(59,130,246,0.4)] transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              {currentStep === steps.length - 1 ? "Start Using Cipher" : "Continue"}
              {currentStep !== steps.length - 1 && <ChevronRight size={18} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=========================================
FILE: src/ndk.js
=========================================

import NDK, { NDKPrivateKeySigner, NDKEvent, NDKRelay } from '@nostr-dev-kit/ndk';

// Patch the NDKRelay class to stringify AUTH for the strict Rust relay
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && !window.__NDK_PATCHED) {
  window.__NDK_PATCHED = true;
  const originalSend = NDKRelay.prototype.send;
  
  NDKRelay.prototype.send = function(msg) {
    if (this.url && this.url.includes('aegispayhq.com')) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(msg);
        if (parsed[0] === 'AUTH' && typeof parsed[1] === 'object') {
          console.log('🛠️ NDK Relay intercepting AUTH event, stringifying...');
          msg = JSON.stringify(['AUTH', JSON.stringify(parsed[1])]);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    return originalSend.call(this, msg);
  };
}

export const getNDK = (secretKey) => {
  const signer = new NDKPrivateKeySigner(secretKey);
  const ndk = new NDK({
    explicitRelayUrls: ['wss://groups.aegispayhq.com', 'wss://relay.primal.net'],
    signer: signer,
  });

  ndk.connect().catch(e => console.warn('[NDK] connect failed:', e));
  return ndk;
};

export { NDKEvent };

FILE: src/Shop.jsx
