import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Compass, Image as ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import OneTapCheckout from './components/OneTapCheckout';
import { useNIP44DM } from './hooks/useNIP44DM';
import { saveMessage } from './db';
import { useChatStore } from './store/chatStore';

export default function Shop({ identity, setActiveTab }) {
  const { sendDM } = useNIP44DM({ identity });
  const setDms = useChatStore((s) => s.setDms);
  const openChat = useChatStore((s) => s.openChat);
  const setToast = useChatStore((s) => s.setToast);
  const [globalProducts, setGlobalProducts] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [checkoutProduct, setCheckoutProduct] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!identity?.pk) return;
    setLoading(true);

    const fetchMarketplace = async () => {
      try {
        const { SimplePool } = await import('nostr-tools');
        const pool = new SimplePool();
        
        // Fetch all NIP-99 product listings (kind 30402)
        const events = await pool.querySync(
          ['wss://groups.aegispayhq.com', 'wss://relay.primal.net'],
          { kinds: [30402], '#t': ['cipher_marketplace'], limit: 100 }
        );

        const parsed = events.map(ev => {
          const getTag = (name) => ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === name)?.[1];
          const priceStr = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'price')?.[1] || '0';
          return {
            id: getTag('d') || ev.id,
            name: getTag('title') || 'Unnamed Product',
            priceUsd: parseFloat(priceStr),
            image: getTag('image') || '',
            vendorPubkey: ev.pubkey
          };
        }).filter(p => p.priceUsd > 0);

        // Deduplicate by product ID
        const unique = Array.from(new Map(parsed.map(p => [p.id, p])).values());
        setGlobalProducts(unique);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch marketplace:", err);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchMarketplace();
  }, [identity?.pk]);

  const handleConfirmOrder = async (order) => {
    console.log('[BUY] 1. handleConfirmOrder fired with:', order);
    console.log('[BUY] ⚠️ CHECK VENDOR PUBKEY:', order.vendorPubkey);
    setCheckoutProduct(null);
    
    const targetDm = order.vendorPubkey;
    console.log('[BUY] 2. targetDm (vendor pubkey):', targetDm);
    if (!targetDm) {
      console.error('[BUY] 3. ABORTING - vendor pubkey missing');
      if (setToast) setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Vendor pubkey missing' });
      return;
    }

    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;
    const payload = { 
      type: 'cipher_order', 
      productName: order.productName, 
      amountFiat: order.amountFiat,
      amountCrypto: order.amountCrypto,
      currency: order.currency,
      shippingAddress: order.shippingAddress || 'No address provided',
      shippingAddress: order.shippingAddress || 'No address provided',
      status: 'payment_sent',
      shippingAddress: order.shippingAddress || 'No address provided'
    };

    try {
      // 1. Save to Dexie
      await saveMessage(targetDm, { 
        id: tempId, 
        clientId: tempId,
        isSender: true, 
        timestamp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), 
        data: payload, 
        pubkey: identity.pk, 
        isGroup: false 
      });

      // 2. Update Zustand Store (Optimistic UI)
      setDms(prev => {
        const chatMsgs = prev[targetDm] || [];
        return { 
          ...prev, 
          [targetDm]: [...chatMsgs, { 
            id: tempId, clientId: tempId, isSender: true, 
            timestamp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), data: payload, pubkey: identity.pk 
          }] 
        };
      });

      // 3. Send DM to Vendor
      console.log('[BUY] 3. Calling sendDM...');
      const sendResult = await sendDM(targetDm, payload, tempId, null);
      console.log('[BUY] 4. sendDM result:', sendResult);

      // 4. Switch to Chat Tab and Open the DM
      console.log('[BUY] 5. Calling openChat + setActiveTab');
      openChat('dm', targetDm);
      if (setActiveTab) setActiveTab('chat');
      console.log('[BUY] 6. DONE - switched to chat tab');
      
      if (setToast) setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Order sent to vendor!' });

    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[BUY] FAILED:", e);
      if (setToast) setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Failed to send order DM' });
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="p-4 h-full relative pb-24 bg-white dark:bg-black overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Compass size={24} className="text-black dark:text-white" />
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Explore</h2>
          </div>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="flex justify-center mt-20">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
          </div>
        ) : globalProducts.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center text-black dark:text-white/40 mt-20">
            <Compass size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 opacity-50" />
            <p>No products found on the network yet.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            {globalProducts.map(p => (
              <div key={p.id} className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col group">
                <div className="relative h-32 w-full cursor-pointer overflow-hidden" onClick={() => setCheckoutProduct(p)}>
                  {p.image ? (
                    <img src={p.image} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full bg-white/5 flex items-center justify-center text-black dark:text-white/20"><ImageIcon size={32} /></div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="p-3 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold truncate text-black dark:text-white">{p.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-green-400 font-bold">${p.priceUsd}</p>
                  </div>
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setCheckoutProduct(p)}
                    className="w-full mt-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-blue-600 text-black dark:text-white text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50 hover:border-blue-500"
                  >
                    Buy Now
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <OneTapCheckout
        isOpen={!!checkoutProduct}
        onClose={() => setCheckoutProduct(null)}
        product={checkoutProduct}
        vendor={{ pubkey: checkoutProduct?.vendorPubkey || checkoutProduct?.pubkey || '', btcAddress: 'bc1qxy2kgdygjrsqtzq2n0yrf2493p83kkfjhx0wlh', xmrAddress: '44AFFq5kSiGBoZ4NMDwYtN18obc8AemS33DBLWs3H7otXft3XjrpDtQGv7SqSsaBYBb98uNbr2VBBEt7f2wfn3RVGQBEP3A' }}
        identity={identity}
        onConfirmOrder={handleConfirmOrder}
      />
    </>
  );
}

=========================================
FILE: src/LockScreen.jsx
=========================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Lock, Fingerprint, ChevronLeft, DownloadCloud, AtSign, Camera, RefreshCw, KeyRound, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { useChatStore } from "./store/chatStore";
import { uploadToBlossom } from '@nostr-wot/blossom';

export default function LockScreen({ identity }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState('create'); // create, unlock, restore, restore_mnemonic, setup_profile
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [mnemonic, setMnemonic] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [enableBio, setEnableBio] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [tempIdentity, setTempIdentity] = useState(null);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(null);
  const [uploadingAvatar, setUploadingAvatar] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (identity.hasBiometric && mode === 'unlock') handleBiometric();
  }, [identity.hasBiometric, mode]);

  const handleBiometric = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await identity.unlockWithBiometric();
    if (!res.success) setError('Biometrics failed. Use your PIN.');
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true); setError('');

    const cleanHandle = handle.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '');
    if (mode === 'create' || mode === 'restore') {
      if (cleanHandle.length < 3) { setError('Handle must be at least 3 letters/numbers'); setLoading(false); return; }
    }
    if (pin.length !== 4) { setError('PIN must be 4 digits'); setLoading(false); return; }

    try {
      if (mode === 'create') {
        const res = await identity.createVault(cleanHandle, pin, enableBio);
        setTempIdentity(res);
        setUsername(cleanHandle);
        setDisplayName(cleanHandle);
        setMode('setup_profile');
      } else if (mode === 'unlock') {
        const res = await identity.unlock(pin);
        if (!res.success) setError(res.error || 'Invalid PIN');
      } else if (mode === 'restore_mnemonic') {
        if (!mnemonic.trim() || mnemonic.trim().split(/\s+/).length < 12) {
          setError('Please enter your full 12-word phrase');
        } else {
          const res = await identity.restoreFromMnemonic(mnemonic, pin);
          if (!res.success) setError(res.error);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || 'Something went wrong');
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleAvatarUpload = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file || !tempIdentity) return;
    setUploadingAvatar(true);
    try {
      const blob = await uploadToBlossom(file, {
        signer: {
          getPublicKey: async () => tempIdentity.pk,
          signEvent: async (event) => {
            const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
            return finalizeEvent(event, tempIdentity.sk);
          }
        },
      });
      setAvatar(blob.url);
    } catch (err) { 
      useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'info', message: 'Upload failed: ' + err.message }); 
    } finally { 
      setUploadingAvatar(false); 
    }
  };

  const handleFinishSetup = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const clean = username.replace('@', '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (clean.length < 3) return setError('Username must be 3+ characters');
    if (!/^[a-z0-9_]+$/.test(clean)) return setError('Only letters, numbers, underscores');
    setLoading(true);
    
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_new_account', 'true'); // Trigger tutorial
    await identity.finalizeLogin(tempIdentity.sk, tempIdentity.pk, tempIdentity.npub, {
      username: clean,
      displayName: displayName.trim() || clean,
      picture: avatar
    });
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const isMnemonicMode = mode === 'restore_mnemonic';

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-[100dvh] overflow-y-auto bg-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 relative">
      
      {/* Subtle top glow */}
      <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-full max-w-md h-64 bg-blue-500/10 blur-[100px] pointer-events-none"></div>

      <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-sm flex flex-col items-center">
        
        {/* Logo */}
        <img 
          src="/icon-192.png" 
          alt="Cipher" 
          className="w-28 h-28 object-contain mb-6 drop-shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(59,130,246,0.3)]" 
        />

        {/* Header */}
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-2 text-center">
          {mode === 'setup_profile' ? 'Your Profile' : mode === 'create' ? 'Welcome to Cipher' : mode === 'unlock' ? 'Enter PIN' : mode === 'restore' ? 'Restore Account' : 'Recovery Phrase'}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-gray-400 text-[15px] text-center mb-8 max-w-[280px] leading-relaxed">
          {mode === 'setup_profile' ? 'Let people know who you are.' 
           : mode === 'create' ? 'Pick a handle and a 4-digit PIN.' 
           : mode === 'unlock' ? 'Enter your 4-digit PIN to unlock.' 
           : mode === 'restore' ? 'Enter your handle and PIN.'
           : 'Enter your 12-word phrase and a new PIN.'}
        </p>

        {/* Glass Card Form */}
        <form onSubmit={mode === 'setup_profile' ? handleFinishSetup : handleSubmit} className="w-full flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a]/80 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-white/10 p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl">
          
          {mode === 'setup_profile' ? (
            <>
              <div className="relative mb-6 group cursor-pointer mx-auto">
                <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border-2 border-dashed border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden transition-all group-hover:border-blue-500/50">
                  {avatar ? (
                    <img src={avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <Camera size={28} className="text-gray-500 group-hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors" />
                  )}
                </div>
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleAvatarUpload} className="absolute inset-0 opacity-0 cursor-pointer" />
                {uploadingAvatar && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full"><RefreshCw size={20} className="animate-spin text-white" /></div>}
              </div>

              <input 
                type="text" 
                placeholder="Display Name (optional)"
                value={displayName}
                onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all mb-4"
              />
              
              <div className="w-full relative mb-6">
                <AtSign size={18} className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="username"
                  value={username}
                  onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, ''))}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl pl-11 pr-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              {isMnemonicMode && (
                <textarea
                  placeholder="apple banana cherry ..."
                  value={mnemonic}
                  onChange={(e) => { setMnemonic(e.target.value); setError('') }}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-5 text-[15px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all mb-4 h-28 resize-none font-mono"
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  autoCorrect="off"
                  spellCheck="false"
                />
              )}

              {(mode === 'create' || mode === 'restore') && (
                <div className="w-full relative mb-4">
                  <AtSign size={18} className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="yourhandle"
                    value={handle}
                    onChange={(e) => { setHandle(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '')); setError('') }}
                    className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl pl-11 pr-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all"
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {!isMnemonicMode && (
                <input
                  type="password"
                  placeholder="4-digit PIN"
                  value={pin}
                  onChange={(e) => { setPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4)); setError('') }}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all tracking-[0.5em] text-center mb-4"
                  maxLength={4}
                />
              )}

              {mode === 'create' && (
                <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 text-gray-400 text-sm mt-2 mb-2 cursor-pointer hover:text-white transition-colors">
                  <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center w-5 h-5">
                    <input type="checkbox" checked={enableBio} onChange={(e) => setEnableBio(e.target.checked)} className="peer appearance-none w-5 h-5 border border-white/20 rounded-md checked:bg-blue-500 checked:border-blue-500 transition-colors cursor-pointer" />
                    <Check size={14} className="absolute text-white pointer-events-none opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100 transition-opacity" strokeWidth={3} />
                  </div>
                  Enable Biometric Unlock
                </label>
              )}
            </>
          )}

          {error && <p className="text-red-500 text-sm text-center mt-2 mb-2 font-medium">{error}</p>}

          <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-semibold text-[16px] tracking-wide py-4 rounded-xl mt-4 shadow-[0_4px_20px_-4px_rgba(59,130,246,0.4)] transition-all active:scale-[0.98] disabled:bg-[#1a1a1a] disabled:text-gray-600 disabled:border disabled:border-white/5 disabled:shadow-none disabled:pointer-events-none flex justify-center items-center">
            {loading ? <RefreshCw size={20} className="animate-spin" /> : mode === 'setup_profile' ? 'Enter Cipher' : mode === 'create' ? 'Create Account' : mode === 'unlock' ? 'Unlock' : mode === 'restore' ? 'Restore Account' : 'Recover Account'}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Footer Links */}
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          {mode === 'create' && (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore'); setError(''); setPin('') }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
              Already have an account? Restore it.
            </button>
          )}

          {mode === 'restore' && (
            <>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore_mnemonic'); setError(''); setPin(''); }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-2">
                <KeyRound size={14} /> Use Recovery Phrase
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('create'); setError(''); setPin('') }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                Create a new account instead
              </button>
            </>
          )}

          {mode === 'restore_mnemonic' && (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore'); setError(''); setPin(''); }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-2">
              <ChevronLeft size={14} /> Back to handle restore
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FILE: src/useIdentity.js
=========================================

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { registerPasskeyIdentity, unlockPasskeyIdentity, isPRFSupported } from 'nostr-passkey'
import * as bip39 from '@scure/bip39'
import { wordlist } from '@scure/bip39/wordlists/english.js'

async function sha256(message) {
  const msgBuffer = new TextEncoder().encode(message)
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgBuffer)
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
  return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
}

async function getKey(secret) {
  const enc = new TextEncoder()
  const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw", enc.encode(secret), { name: "PBKDF2" }, false, ["deriveKey"]
  )
  return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: "PBKDF2", salt: enc.encode("cipher_secure_salt"), iterations: 100000, hash: "SHA-256" },
    keyMaterial, { name: "AES-GCM", length: 256 }, false, ["encrypt", "decrypt"]
  )
}

export async function encryptKey(skArray, secret) {
  const key = await getKey(secret)
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12))
  const enc = new TextEncoder()
  const encrypted = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: "AES-GCM", iv }, key, enc.encode(JSON.stringify(skArray))
  )
  return { iv: Array.from(iv), data: Array.from(new Uint8Array(encrypted)) }
}

export async function decryptKey(encryptedObj, secret) {
  const key = await getKey(secret)
  const decrypted = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
    { name: "AES-GCM", iv: new Uint8Array(encryptedObj.iv) }, key, new Uint8Array(encryptedObj.data)
  )
  const dec = new TextDecoder()
  return JSON.parse(dec.decode(decrypted))
}

export function useIdentity() {
  const [keys, setKeys] = useState({ sk: null, pk: null, npub: null })
  const [hasVault, setHasVault] = useState(false)
  const [hasBiometric, setHasBiometric] = useState(false)
  const [loadingSession, setLoadingSession] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const init = async () => {
      const sessionSk = sessionStorage.getItem('cipher_session_sk')
      if (sessionSk) {
        try {
          const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
          const sk = Uint8Array.from(JSON.parse(sessionSk))
          const pk = getPublicKey(sk)
          setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) })
          setHasVault(true)
          setLoadingSession(false)
          return
        } catch (e) { sessionStorage.removeItem('cipher_session_sk') }
      }

      const vault = localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault')
      if (vault) setHasVault(true)

      const bioEnabled = localStorage.getItem('cipher_biometric_enabled')
      if (bioEnabled === 'true') {
        const supported = await isPRFSupported()
        if (supported) setHasBiometric(true)
      }
      
      setLoadingSession(false)
    }
    init()
  }, [])

  const createVault = async (handle, pin) => {
    const { generateSecretKey, getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
    const sk = generateSecretKey()
    const pk = getPublicKey(sk)
    
    // Use the handle (lowercased) as the lookup ID for restoring
    const lookupId = await sha256(handle.toLowerCase().trim())

    // Encrypt with PIN locally
    const encryptedLocal = await encryptKey(Array.from(sk), pin)
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(encryptedLocal))
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_handle', handle.toLowerCase().trim())
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', '0')

    // Encrypt with PIN and back up to relays (so restore works with handle + PIN)
    try {
      const { SimplePool, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const encryptedBackup = await encryptKey(Array.from(sk), pin)
      const pool = new SimplePool()
      const relays = ['wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://relay.damus.io', 'wss://relay.nostr.band']

      const eventTemplate = {
        kind: 30078,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['d', lookupId]],
        content: JSON.stringify(encryptedBackup),
      }

      const signedEvent = finalizeEvent(eventTemplate, sk)
      const publishPromises = pool.publish(relays, signedEvent)
      const timeout = new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 3000))
      await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled(publishPromises), timeout])
    } catch (e) { console.error("Backup failed:", e) }

    return { sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk), handle }
  }

  // UPDATED: Accepts a full profile object
  const finalizeLogin = async (sk, pk, npub, profile) => {
    try {
      const { SimplePool, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const pool = new SimplePool()
      const relays = ['wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://relay.damus.io', 'wss://relay.nostr.band']

      const profileData = {
        name: profile.username, // @handle (e.g. "alice")
        display_name: profile.displayName || profile.username, // "Alice's Shop"
        about: 'New to Cipher.',
        picture: profile.picture || null
      }

      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 0, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), tags: [],
        content: JSON.stringify(profileData)
      }, sk)

      const publishPromises = pool.publish(relays, ev)
      const timeout = new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 3000))
      await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled(publishPromises), timeout])
    } catch (e) { console.error("Failed to publish initial profile", e) }

    sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
    setKeys({ sk, pk, npub })
    setHasVault(true)
  }

  const unlock = async (pin) => {
    try {
      const vaultStr = localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault')
      if (!vaultStr) throw new Error("No local vault found")
      
      const vault = JSON.parse(vaultStr)
      const skArray = await decryptKey(vault, pin)
      
      const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(skArray)
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk)
      
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', '0')
      setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) })
      return { success: true }
    } catch (e) {
      const fails = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('cipher_fails') || '0') + 1
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', fails.toString())
      if (fails >= 5) { localStorage.clear(); sessionStorage.clear(); window.location.reload() }
      return { success: false, remaining: 5 - fails }
    }
  }

  const unlockWithBiometric = async () => {
    try {
      const { secretKey, pubkey } = await unlockPasskeyIdentity()
      const { nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(secretKey)
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
      setKeys({ sk, pk: pubkey, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pubkey) })
      return { success: true }
    } catch (e) { return { success: false, error: e.message } }
  }

  const restoreFromRelays = async (handle, pin) => {
    try {
      const normalizedHandle = handle.toLowerCase().trim()
      const lookupId = await sha256(normalizedHandle)
      const { SimplePool } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const pool = new SimplePool()
      const relays = ['wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://relay.damus.io', 'wss://relay.nostr.band']
      
      const events = await pool.querySync(relays, { kinds: [30078], '#d': [lookupId] })

      if (events.length === 0) throw new Error("No account found for that handle.")
      
      events.sort((a, b) => b.created_at - a.created_at)
      const encryptedBackup = JSON.parse(events[0].content)

      const skArray = await decryptKey(encryptedBackup, pin)
      
      const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(skArray)
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk)

      const newLocalVault = await encryptKey(Array.from(sk), pin)
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(newLocalVault))
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_handle', normalizedHandle)
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', '0')
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
      
      setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) })
      setHasVault(true)
      return { success: true }
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message }
    }
  }


  const restoreFromMnemonic = async (mnemonicInput, newPin) => {
    try {
      const normalizedMnemonic = mnemonicInput.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      if (!bip39.validateMnemonic(normalizedMnemonic, wordlist)) {
        throw new Error("Invalid 12-word phrase. Please check your words.")
      }

      const lookupId = await sha256(normalizedMnemonic)
      const { SimplePool } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const pool = new SimplePool()
      const relays = ['wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://relay.damus.io', 'wss://relay.nostr.band']

      const events = await pool.querySync(relays, { kinds: [30078], '#d': [lookupId] })
      if (events.length === 0) throw new Error("No backup found for this phrase.")

      events.sort((a, b) => b.created_at - a.created_at)
      const encryptedBackup = JSON.parse(events[0].content)

      const skArray = await decryptKey(encryptedBackup, normalizedMnemonic)

      const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(skArray)
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk)

      const newLocalVault = await encryptKey(Array.from(sk), newPin)
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(newLocalVault))
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', '0')
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))

      setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) })
      setHasVault(true)
      return { success: true }
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message }
    }
  }

  const lockApp = () => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem('cipher_session_sk')
    setKeys({ sk: null, pk: null, npub: null })
  }

  const panicWipe = () => {
    localStorage.clear()
    sessionStorage.clear()
    window.location.reload()
  }

  return { 
    ...keys, hasVault, hasBiometric, loadingSession, 
    unlock, unlockWithBiometric, restoreFromRelays, restoreFromMnemonic, createVault, finalizeLogin, lockApp, panicWipe
  }
}

=========================================
FILE: src/hooks/useNIP29Group.js
=========================================

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { groupRelays, generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';
import { saveMessage } from '../db';
import { getMyGroups, setMyGroups } from '../lib/myGroups';
import { encodeGroupPayload, decodeGroupEvent } from '../lib/crypto/groupMessageCodec';
import { putGroupKey, getAllGroupKeys, getCurrentGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyStore';
import { generateGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupCipher';
import { distributeGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyDistribution';

export function useNIP29Group({ identity }) {
  const setGroupsStore = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroups);
  const setGroups = useCallback((val) => {
    setGroupsStore((prev) => {
      const next = typeof val === 'function' ? val(prev) : val;
      // Strict filter: only allow groups the user has explicitly joined/created.
      // This kills the relay WS listener that injects "lmfao".
      const myGroupIds = new Set(getMyGroups(identity?.pk).map(g => g.id));
      return next.filter(g => myGroupIds.has(g.id));
    });
  }, [identity?.pk, setGroupsStore]);
  const setGroupMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroupMessages);
  const setGroupMembers = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroupMembers);
  const setGroupTypingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroupTypingUsers);
  const setPinnedMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.setPinnedMessages);
  const activeGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.activeGroup);

  const sortMessages = (a, b) => {
    const tA = a.local_timestamp || (a.timestamp * 1000);
    const tB = b.local_timestamp || (b.timestamp * 1000);
    if (tA !== tB) return tA - tB;
    return (a.id || '').localeCompare(b.id || '');
  };

  // CREATE GROUP
  const createGroup = useCallback(async (name, about, isChannel) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const groupId = (globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() || Date.now().toString()).replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 16);

    const createEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9007, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['name', name], ['about', about || ''], ['open'], ['p', identity.pk, 'admin']],
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);

    const r9007 = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, createEv));
    const ok = r9007.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled');
    if (!ok) throw new Error('No relay accepted 9007');

    const metaTags = [['h', groupId], ['name', name], ['about', about || '']];
    if (isChannel) {
      metaTags.push(['channel', 'true']);
      metaTags.push(['broadcast', 'true']);
    }
    
    const metaEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9002, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: metaTags,
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, metaEv));

    const joinEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9021, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId]], content: 'Creator'
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, joinEv));

    try {
      const existing = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
      if (!existing) {
        const key = generateGroupKey();
        await putGroupKey(groupId, 1, key);
        await distributeGroupKey({
          senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk,
          groupId, epoch: 1, keyBytes: key, recipients: [identity.pk],
          publish: async (dmEv) => {
            const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, dmEv));
            if (!results.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')) throw new Error('dm rejected');
          },
        });
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[createGroup] key bootstrap deferred', e); }

    const newGroup = { id: groupId, name, about: about || '', isChannel, creator: identity.pk, members: [identity.pk] };

    const localGroups = getMyGroups(identity.pk);
    if (!localGroups.find(g => g.id === groupId)) {
      localGroups.push(newGroup);
      setMyGroups(identity.pk, localGroups);
    }
    setGroups(prev => [newGroup, ...(prev || []).filter(g => g.id !== groupId)]);

    return newGroup;
  }, [identity, setGroups]);

  // JOIN GROUP
  const joinGroupById = useCallback(async (groupId, passedName = null) => {
    if (!groupId) return;
    let realName = passedName || 'Unnamed Group';

    const localGroups = getMyGroups(identity.pk);
    const localMatch = localGroups.find(g => g.id === groupId);
    if (!passedName && localMatch?.name && localMatch.name !== 'Unnamed Group') {
      realName = localMatch.name;
    } else if (!passedName) {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [9002], limit: 200 }, 3000);
        const match = events.find(ev => ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'h')?.[1] === groupId);
        if (match) {
          const nameTag = match.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'name')?.[1];
          if (nameTag) realName = nameTag;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    if (realName === 'Unnamed Group') {
      const userInput = window.prompt('What is this group called?', '');
      if (userInput && userInput.trim()) realName = userInput.trim();
    }

    const newGroup = { id: groupId, name: realName, isChannel: false, creator: '' };
    const idx = localGroups.findIndex(g => g.id === groupId);
    if (idx === -1) localGroups.push(newGroup);
    else localGroups[idx].name = realName;
    setMyGroups(identity.pk, localGroups);
    setGroups(prev => [newGroup, ...(prev || []).filter(g => g.id !== groupId)]);

    return newGroup;
  }, [identity, setGroups]);

  // LEAVE GROUP
  const leaveGroup = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const leaveEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9022, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId]], content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, leaveEv));

    const localGroups = getMyGroups(identity.pk);
    const updated = localGroups.filter(g => g.id !== groupId);
    setMyGroups(identity.pk, updated);
    setGroups(updated);
  }, [identity, setGroups]);

  // SEND GROUP MESSAGE
  const sendGroupMessage = useCallback(async (groupId, payload, clientId = null, replyId = null) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const encoded = await encodeGroupPayload({ groupId, payload, senderPubkey: identity.pk, waitForKey: true });
    if (encoded.pending) {
      try {
        const reqEv = finalizeEvent({
          kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
          tags: [['h', groupId]],
          content: JSON.stringify({ type: 'gkey_req' })
        }, identity.sk);
        pool.publish(groupRelays, reqEv);
      } catch (e) {}
      return { success: false, error: 'NO_KEY' };
    }
    const tags = [['h', groupId], ...encoded.tags];
    if (clientId) tags.push(['client', clientId]);
    if (replyId) tags.push(['e', replyId, '', 'reply']);
    const event = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags, content: encoded.content
    }, identity.sk);
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, event));
    return { success: true, event, results };
  }, [identity]);

  // SEND GROUP TYPING INDICATOR
  const requestGroupKey = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['h', groupId]], content: JSON.stringify({ type: 'gkey_req' })
      }, identity.sk);
      pool.publish(groupRelays, ev);
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [identity]);

  const sendGroupTypingIndicator = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const encoded = await encodeGroupPayload({ groupId, payload: { type: 'typing' }, senderPubkey: identity.pk });
      if (encoded.pending) return;
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['h', groupId], ...encoded.tags], content: encoded.content
      }, identity.sk);
      pool.publish(groupRelays, ev);
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [identity]);

  // ADMIN ACTIONS
  const kickMember = useCallback(async (groupId, memberPubkey) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const allMembers = useChatStore.getState().groupMembers || [];
    const remaining = allMembers.filter(m => m.pubkey !== memberPubkey).map(m => m.pubkey);
    if (!remaining.includes(identity.pk)) remaining.push(identity.pk);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9001, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['p', memberPubkey]], content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setGroupMembers(prev => prev.filter(m => m.pubkey !== memberPubkey));
    try {
      const current = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
      if (!current) return;
      const nextEpoch = current.epoch + 1;
      const newKey = generateGroupKey();
      await putGroupKey(groupId, nextEpoch, newKey);
      const priorEpochs = (await getAllGroupKeys(groupId)).filter(e => e.epoch !== nextEpoch);
      await distributeGroupKey({
        senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk,
        groupId, epoch: nextEpoch, keyBytes: newKey,
        recipients: remaining, extraEpochs: priorEpochs,
        publish: async (dmEv) => {
          const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, dmEv));
          if (!results.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')) throw new Error('dm rejected');
        },
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[kickMember] rotation deferred', e);
    }
  }, [identity, setGroupMembers]);

  const promoteMember = useCallback(async (groupId, memberPubkey, isAdmin) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9000, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['p', memberPubkey], ['role', isAdmin ? 'member' : 'admin']],
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setGroupMembers(prev => prev.map(m => m.pubkey === memberPubkey ? { ...m, role: isAdmin ? 'member' : 'admin' } : m));
  }, [identity, setGroupMembers]);

  // ADD MEMBER
  const addMember = useCallback(async (groupId, memberPubkey) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9000, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['p', memberPubkey]], content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setGroupMembers(prev => {
      if (prev.findIndex(x => x.pubkey === memberPubkey)) {
        return [...prev, { pubkey: memberPubkey, role: 'member' }];
      }
      return prev;
    });
  }, [identity, setGroupMembers]);

  // FETCH MEMBERS
  const fetchGroupMembers = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        const evs = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [39002], '#d': [groupId], '#h': [groupId] });
        if (evs.length) {
          const t = evs[0].tags.filter(x => x[0] === 'p');
          const m = t.map(x => ({ pubkey: x[1], role: x[2] || 'member' }));
          if (!m.find(x => x.pubkey === identity.pk)) {
            m.push({ pubkey: identity.pk, role: activeGroup?.creator === identity.pk ? 'admin' : 'member' });
          }
          setGroupMembers(m);
          return m.map(x => x.pubkey);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[useNIP29Group] fetchGroupMembers failed:', err);
      }
      
      // Fallback: If relay returns no 39002, at least show the current user
      const fallback = [{ pubkey: identity.pk, role: activeGroup?.creator === identity.pk ? 'admin' : 'member' }];
      setGroupMembers(fallback);
      return [identity.pk];
    }, [identity, activeGroup, setGroupMembers]);

  // RAW WEBSOCKET SUBSCRIPTION (NIP-42 AUTH + join + subscribe)
  const subscribeToGroups = useCallback((groupIds, since) => {
    if (!groupIds || groupIds.length === 0) return () => {};

    let ws;
    let authSent = false;
    let sentReq = false;
    let isMounted = true;
    let keyHandler = null;

    const connect = () => {
      ws = new WebSocket('wss://groups.aegispayhq.com');

      ws.onopen = () => console.log('✅ Group WS connected');

      ws.onmessage = async (e) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        const msg = JSON.parse(e.data);

        if (msg[0] === 'AUTH') {
          if (authSent) return;
          authSent = true;
          const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
          const challenge = typeof msg[1] === 'string' ? msg[1] : msg[1].challenge;
          const authEv = finalizeEvent({
            kind: 22242, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
            tags: [['relay', 'wss://groups.aegispayhq.com'], ['challenge', challenge]],
            content: ''
          }, identity.sk);
          ws.send(JSON.stringify(['AUTH', authEv]));
          console.log('➡️ Group WS sent AUTH');
        }
        else if (msg[0] === 'OK' && msg[2] === true && !sentReq) {
          sentReq = true;
          console.log('✅ Group WS Auth OK');
          const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');

          for (const gid of groupIds) {
            const adminEv = finalizeEvent({
              kind: 9000, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
              tags: [['h', gid], ['p', identity.pk], ['role', 'admin']], content: ''
            }, identity.sk);
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(['EVENT', adminEv]));
          }

          setTimeout(() => {
            if (!isMounted) return;
            for (const gid of groupIds) {
              const joinEv = finalizeEvent({
                kind: 9021, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
                tags: [['h', gid]], content: 'Auto-join'
              }, identity.sk);
              ws.send(JSON.stringify(['EVENT', joinEv]));
            }

            setTimeout(() => {
              if (!isMounted) return;
              for (const gid of groupIds) {
                ws.send(JSON.stringify(['REQ', `group-sub-${gid}`, { kinds: [7, 9, 39002, 9021, 9000], '#h': [gid], since }]));
              }
              console.log('✅ Group WS sent individual REQs');
            }, 1500);
          }, 1000);
        }
        else if (msg[0] === 'EVENT' && msg[1].startsWith('group-sub-')) {
          const event = msg[2];
          const hTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'h');
          if (!hTag) return;
          const groupId = hTag[1];

          if (event.kind === 9021 || event.kind === 9000) {
            const isJoin = event.kind === 9021;
            const pTags = event.tags.filter(t => t[0] === 'p').map(t => t[1]);
            const newPubkeys = isJoin ? [event.pubkey] : pTags;
            
            setGroupMembers(prev => {
              let updated = Array.isArray(prev) ? [...prev] : [];
              let changed = false;
              for (const pk of newPubkeys) {
                if (updated.findIndex(m => m.pubkey === pk)) {
                  updated.push({ pubkey: pk, role: 'member' });
                  changed = true;
                }
              }
              return changed ? updated : prev;
            });
            return;
          }

          if (event.kind === 39002) {
            const t = event.tags.filter(x => x[0] === 'p');
            const m = t.map(x => ({ pubkey: x[1], role: x[2] || 'member' }));
            if (!m.find(x => x.pubkey === identity.pk)) {
              m.push({ pubkey: identity.pk, role: 'member' });
            }
            setGroupMembers(m);
            return;
          }

          // Handle Reaction Events
          if (event.kind === 7) {
            const targetId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e')?.[1];
            if (targetId) {
              useChatStore.getState().setReactions(prev => {
                const existing = prev[targetId] || [];
                if (existing.find(r => r.pubkey === event.pubkey && r.emoji === event.content)) return prev;
                return { ...prev, [targetId]: [...existing, { emoji: event.content, pubkey: event.pubkey }] };
              });
            }
            return;
          }

          let isGkeyReq = false;
          try {
            const parsedQuick = JSON.parse(event.content);
            if (parsedQuick.type === 'gkey_req') {
              isGkeyReq = true;
              if (event.pubkey !== identity.pk) {
                const current = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
                if (current) {
                  const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
                  await distributeGroupKey({
                    senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk,
                    groupId, epoch: current.epoch, keyBytes: current.bytes,
                    recipients: [event.pubkey],
                    publish: async (dmEv) => {
                      const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, dmEv));
                      if (!results.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')) throw new Error('dm rejected');
                    },
                  });
                }
              }
            }
          } catch (e) {}
          if (isGkeyReq) return;

          let parsed = {};
          let plaintext = event.content;
          let isPending = false;
          let decEpoch = null;
          const decoded = await decodeGroupEvent({ event, groupId });
          if (decoded.pending) { isPending = true; decEpoch = decoded.epoch; }
          else if (decoded.error) { console.warn('[group-decrypt] failed', decoded.reason); return; }
          else { plaintext = decoded.plaintext; parsed = decoded.parsed || {}; decEpoch = decoded.epoch; }

          if (!isPending && parsed.type === 'typing') {
            const age = (Date.now() / 1000) - event.created_at;
            if (age < 10 && event.pubkey !== identity.pk) {
              setGroupTypingUsers(prev => ({ ...prev, [event.pubkey]: Date.now() }));
            }
            return;
          }

          // Safety net: If a message comes from someone not in the list, add them
          if (event.pubkey !== identity.pk) {
            setGroupMembers(prev => {
              if (prev.findIndex(m => m.pubkey === event.pubkey)) {
                return [...prev, { pubkey: event.pubkey, role: 'member' }];
              }
              return prev;
            });
          }

          const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
          const clientId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];

          setGroupMessages(prev => {
            const groupMsgs = prev[groupId] || [];
            const existingIndex = groupMsgs.findIndex(m => {
              if (clientId && m.clientId === clientId) return true;
              if (m.isSender === (event.pubkey === identity.pk) && !isPending && parsed.message) {
                let oldData = {};
                try { oldData = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}'); } catch(e) {}
                return oldData.message === parsed.message && Math.abs(m.timestamp - event.created_at) < 5;
              }
              return false;
            });

            const baseMsg = {
              id: event.id, clientId, isSender: event.pubkey === identity.pk,
              pubkey: event.pubkey, timestamp: event.created_at,
              local_timestamp: Date.now(),
              rawContent: isPending ? null : plaintext,
              ciphertext: isPending ? event.content : null,
              epoch: decEpoch, pending: isPending,
              replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null, isGroup: true
            };

            if (existingIndex >= 0) {
              const newMsgs = [...groupMsgs];
              newMsgs[existingIndex] = { ...newMsgs[existingIndex], ...baseMsg };
              saveMessage(groupId, newMsgs[existingIndex]);
              return { ...prev, [groupId]: newMsgs };
            } else {
              saveMessage(groupId, baseMsg);
              return { ...prev, [groupId]: [...groupMsgs, baseMsg].sort(sortMessages) };
            }
          });
        }
        else if (msg[0] === 'CLOSED') {
          console.log('❌ Group WS closed:', msg[2]);
        }
      };

      if (keyHandler) window.removeEventListener('cipher:group-key-updated', keyHandler);
      keyHandler = async (e) => {
        const { groupId: gid } = e.detail;
        if (!groupIds.includes(gid)) return;
        try {
          const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
          const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [9], '#h': [gid], limit: 100 }, 4000);
          for (const event of events) {
            const decoded = await decodeGroupEvent({ event, groupId: gid });
            if (decoded.pending || decoded.error) continue;
            const parsed = decoded.parsed || {};
            if (parsed.type === 'typing') continue;
            const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
            const clientId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];
            setGroupMessages(prev => {
              const groupMsgs = prev[gid] || [];
              const idx = groupMsgs.findIndex(m => m.id === event.id);
              const newMsg = {
                id: event.id, clientId, isSender: event.pubkey === identity.pk,
                pubkey: event.pubkey, timestamp: event.created_at,
                local_timestamp: Date.now(), rawContent: decoded.plaintext,
                epoch: decoded.epoch, pending: false,
                replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null, isGroup: true,
              };
              if (idx >= 0) { const u = [...groupMsgs]; u[idx] = { ...u[idx], ...newMsg }; return { ...prev, [gid]: u }; }
              return { ...prev, [gid]: [...groupMsgs, newMsg].sort(sortMessages) };
            });
          }
        } catch (err) { console.warn('[group-key] refresh failed', err); }
      };
      window.addEventListener('cipher:group-key-updated', keyHandler);

      ws.onclose = (e) => {
        console.log('❌ Group WS closed:', e.reason);
        if (isMounted) setTimeout(connect, 3000);
      };

      ws.onerror = (e) => console.error('Group WS error:', e);
    };

    connect();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      if (keyHandler) window.removeEventListener('cipher:group-key-updated', keyHandler);
      if (ws) ws.close();
    };
  }, [identity, setGroupMessages, setGroupTypingUsers]);

  const pinMessage = useCallback(async (groupId, messageEventId) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9005,
      created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['e', messageEventId]],
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: messageEventId }));
    return { success: true };
  }, [identity, setPinnedMessages]);

  const unpinMessage = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9005,
      created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId]],
      content: 'unpin'
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: null }));
    return { success: true };
  }, [identity, setPinnedMessages]);

  const fetchPinnedMessage = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    try {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [9005], '#h': [groupId], limit: 10 }, 3000);
      if (events.length === 0) {
        setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: null }));
        return null;
      }
      events.sort((a, b) => b.created_at - a.created_at);
      const eTag = events[0].tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e')?.[1];
      setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: eTag || null }));
      return eTag || null;
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }, [identity, setPinnedMessages]);

  return {
    createGroup,
    joinGroupById,
    addMember,
    leaveGroup,
    sendGroupMessage,
    sendGroupTypingIndicator,
    requestGroupKey,
    kickMember,
    promoteMember,
    fetchGroupMembers,
    subscribeToGroups,
    pinMessage,
    unpinMessage,
    fetchPinnedMessage,
  };
}

FILE: src/hooks/usePanicWipe.js
=========================================

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { db } from '../db';

export function usePanicWipe(identity, enabled) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    let lastX = null, lastY = null, lastZ = null;
    let lastTime = 0;
    let spikeCount = 0;
    let lastSpikeTime = 0;
    // Critical: ignore motion for 3 seconds after toggling, so the tap itself doesn't wipe
    const ARM_DELAY = 3000;
    const armTime = Date.now() + ARM_DELAY;

    // Thresholds (tuned for gravity-included accelerometer, m/s²)
    const SHAKE_THRESHOLD = 35;     // magnitude of delta required for a spike
    const SPIKE_WINDOW = 1000;      // spikes must occur within this window
    const REQUIRED_SPIKES = 3;      // must detect this many spikes in the window
    const MIN_EVENT_INTERVAL = 50;  // throttle to ~20Hz

    const handleMotion = (event) => {
      // Still arming — ignore all motion
      if (Date.now() < armTime) return;

      const a = event.accelerationIncludingGravity || event.acceleration;
      if (!a || a.x === null || a.y === null || a.z === null) return;

      const now = Date.now();
      if (now - lastTime < MIN_EVENT_INTERVAL) return;
      lastTime = now;

      if (lastX !== null) {
        const dX = Math.abs(a.x - lastX);
        const dY = Math.abs(a.y - lastY);
        const dZ = Math.abs(a.z - lastZ);
        const magnitude = dX + dY + dZ;

        if (magnitude > SHAKE_THRESHOLD) {
          // Reset counter if last spike was too long ago
          if (now - lastSpikeTime > SPIKE_WINDOW) spikeCount = 0;
          spikeCount++;
          lastSpikeTime = now;

          if (spikeCount >= REQUIRED_SPIKES) {
            triggerWipe();
          }
        }
      }

      lastX = a.x;
      lastY = a.y;
      lastZ = a.z;
    };

    const triggerWipe = async () => {
      if (window.__PANIC_WIPING) return;
      window.__PANIC_WIPING = true;

      // Vibrate to confirm (three buzzes)
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 50, 100, 50, 300]);

      try {
        sessionStorage.clear();
        localStorage.clear();
        // Attempt DB delete with timeout — don't hang forever
        await Promise.race([
          db.delete().catch(() => {}),
          new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800)),
        ]);
      } catch (e) {
        // ignore
      }

      // Reload to lock screen (all data gone)
      window.location.reload();
    };

    const setup = async () => {
      // iOS 13+ requires explicit permission for motion events
      if (
        typeof DeviceMotionEvent !== 'undefined' &&
        typeof DeviceMotionEvent.requestPermission === 'function'
      ) {
        try {
          const permission = await DeviceMotionEvent.requestPermission();
          if (permission !== 'granted') {
            console.warn('Motion permission denied — panic wipe disabled');
            return;
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('Motion permission request failed:', e);
          return;
        }
      }
      window.addEventListener('devicemotion', handleMotion);
    };

    setup();

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('devicemotion', handleMotion);
    };
  }, [enabled, identity]);
}

=========================================
FILE: src/hooks/useReputation.js
=========================================

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';

// Defense layer cache — profile timestamps keyed by pubkey
// Prevents re-fetching the same profile on every modal open
const PROFILE_CACHE_KEY = 'cipher_rep_profile_cache';
const PROFILE_CACHE_TTL = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24h
const MIN_VOUCHER_AGE_DAYS = 7;

function loadProfileCache() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PROFILE_CACHE_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
  } catch { return {}; }
}

function saveProfileCache(cache) {
  try { localStorage.setItem(PROFILE_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cache)); } catch {}
}

export function useReputation({ identity }) {
  const [reputation, setReputation] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const fetchReputation = useCallback(async (targetPubkey) => {
    if (!targetPubkey) return;
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      // 1. Fetch raw vouches + reviews
      const [rawVouches, reviews] = await Promise.all([
        queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [10004], '#p': [targetPubkey] }, 3000),
        queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [10005], '#p': [targetPubkey] }, 3000)
      ]);

      // 2. Defense Layer 1: reject self-vouches + duplicate vouches
      const seenVouchers = new Set();
      const vouchesAfterSelf = rawVouches.filter((ev) => {
        if (ev.pubkey === targetPubkey) return false; // self-vouch
        if (seenVouchers.has(ev.pubkey)) return false; // duplicate
        seenVouchers.add(ev.pubkey);
        return true;
      });

      // 3. Defense Layer 3: check voucher profile age
      const cache = loadProfileCache();
      const now = Date.now();
      const cachedPubkeys = Object.keys(cache);

      // Find which vouchers need profile fetch (not cached, or cache expired)
      const toFetch = vouchesAfterSelf
        .map((v) => v.pubkey)
        .filter((pk) => {
          const c = cache[pk];
          return !c || (now - c.fetchedAt) > PROFILE_CACHE_TTL;
        });

      let freshProfiles = [];
      if (toFetch.length > 0) {
        try {
          freshProfiles = await queryWithTimeout(
            pool,
            generalRelays,
            { kinds: [0], authors: toFetch },
            3000
          );
        } catch {}
      }

      // Merge into cache
      for (const ev of freshProfiles) {
        let meta = {};
        try { meta = JSON.parse(ev.content); } catch {}
        cache[ev.pubkey] = {
          createdAt: ev.created_at,          // profile's last update time
          fetchedAt: now,
          name: meta.display_name || meta.name || ev.pubkey.slice(0, 6),
          picture: meta.picture || null,
        };
      }
      saveProfileCache(cache);

      // 4. Filter vouches by age + build voucher display objects
      const minAgeSec = MIN_VOUCHER_AGE_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60;
      const nowSec = Math.floor(now / 1000);
      const validVouchers = [];
      const filteredVouchers = [];

      for (const ev of vouchesAfterSelf) {
        const profile = cache[ev.pubkey];
        const ageSec = profile ? (nowSec - profile.createdAt) : 0;
        const valid = profile && ageSec >= minAgeSec;

        const info = {
          pubkey: ev.pubkey,
          name: profile?.name || `User ${ev.pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`,
          picture: profile?.picture || null,
          ageDays: Math.floor(ageSec / 86400),
          valid,
          reason: !profile ? 'no-profile' : (ageSec < minAgeSec ? 'too-new' : null),
        };

        if (valid) validVouchers.push(info);
        else filteredVouchers.push(info);
      }

      // 5. Reviews (also self-review protected)
      const validReviews = reviews.filter((ev) => ev.pubkey !== targetPubkey);
      let avgRating = 0;
      if (validReviews.length > 0) {
        const sum = validReviews.reduce(
          (acc, ev) => acc + parseInt(ev.tags.find((t) => t[0] === 'rating')?.[1] || '0'),
          0
        );
        avgRating = (sum / validReviews.length).toFixed(1);
      }

      const hasVouched = vouchesAfterSelf.some((ev) => ev.pubkey === identity.pk);

      setReputation({
        // Trusted count only includes age-validated vouchers
        vouchCount: validVouchers.length,
        rawVouchCount: rawVouches.length,
        filteredCount: filteredVouchers.length,
        vouchList: validVouchers,
        filteredList: filteredVouchers,
        reviewCount: validReviews.length,
        avgRating,
        hasVouched,
        recentReviews: validReviews.slice(0, 3).map((ev) => ({
          pubkey: ev.pubkey,
          rating: parseInt(ev.tags.find((t) => t[0] === 'rating')?.[1] || '0'),
          text: ev.content,
        })),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[useReputation] fetch failed:', e);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [identity]);

  const sendVouch = useCallback(async (targetPubkey) => {
    // Defense Layer 1: refuse to vouch yourself
    if (targetPubkey === identity.pk) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Cannot vouch for yourself' };
    }

    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 10004,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['p', targetPubkey], ['vouch', 'true']],
        content: 'Vouched via Cipher',
      }, identity.sk);

      await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, ev));
      // Refetch to recalculate trust (invalid vouches still don't count)
      await fetchReputation(targetPubkey);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message };
    }
  }, [identity, fetchReputation]);

  const sendReview = useCallback(async (targetPubkey, rating, text) => {
    if (targetPubkey === identity.pk) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Cannot review yourself' };
    }
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 10005,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['p', targetPubkey], ['rating', rating.toString()]],
        content: text,
      }, identity.sk);
      await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, ev));
      await fetchReputation(targetPubkey);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message };
    }
  }, [identity, fetchReputation]);

  return { reputation, loading, fetchReputation, sendVouch, sendReview };
}

FILE: src/hooks/useGroupKeyMember.js
=========================================

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays } from '../lib/relays';
import { fromHex } from '../lib/crypto/groupCipher';
import { hasGroupKey, putGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyStore';
export function useGroupKeyMember({ identity }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!identity?.sk || !identity?.pk) return;
    let sub = null; let mounted = true;
    (async () => {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      sub = pool.subscribe(generalRelays, { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk] }, {
        onevent: async (event) => {
          if (!mounted) return;
          try {
            const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
            const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, event.pubkey);
            const plaintext = nip44.v2.decrypt(event.content, shared);
            const payload = JSON.parse(plaintext);
            if (payload?.t !== 'gkey' || !Array.isArray(payload.keys)) return;
            const { gid } = payload;
            let changed = false;
            for (const item of payload.keys) {
              if (await hasGroupKey(gid, item.ep)) continue;
              await putGroupKey(gid, item.ep, fromHex(item.k), { source: 'dm', from: event.pubkey });
              changed = true;
            }
            if (changed) window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('cipher:group-key-updated', { detail: { groupId: gid } }));
          } catch {}
        },
      });
    })();
    return () => { mounted = false; if (sub) try { sub.close(); } catch {} };
  }, [identity?.pk, identity?.sk]);
}

FILE: src/hooks/useVendorStats.js

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { db } from '../db';

export function useVendorStats({ identity, targetPubkey = null }) {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ sales: 0, revenueFiat: 0, btc: 0, xmr: 0, badges: [] });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const fetchStats = useCallback(async () => {
    const pkToFetch = targetPubkey || identity?.pk;
    if (!pkToFetch) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const allMsgs = await db.messages.toArray();
      
      const completedIds = new Set();
      allMsgs.forEach(m => {
        if (m.data?.type === 'order_update' && m.data?.status === 'completed') {
          completedIds.add(m.data.orderId);
        }
      });

      const orders = allMsgs.filter(m => m.data?.type === 'cipher_order');
      
      let sales = 0;
      let revenueFiat = 0;
      let btc = 0;
      let xmr = 0;

      orders.forEach(o => {
        const isCompleted = completedIds.has(o.id) || o.data.status === 'completed';
        if (isCompleted) {
          sales++;
          revenueFiat += parseFloat(o.data.amountFiat || '0');
          const cryptoAmt = parseFloat(o.data.amountCrypto || '0');
          const currency = (o.data.currency || 'USD').toUpperCase();
          if (currency === 'BTC') btc += cryptoAmt;
          if (currency === 'XMR') xmr += cryptoAmt;
        }
      });

      const badges = [];
      if (sales >= 1) badges.push({ id: 'first_sale', name: 'First Sale', icon: '🏆', color: 'text-yellow-400' });
      if (sales >= 100) badges.push({ id: 'diamond_hands', name: 'Diamond Hands', icon: '💎', color: 'text-blue-400' });
      if (revenueFiat >= 1000) badges.push({ id: 'high_roller', name: '$1K Volume', icon: '💰', color: 'text-green-400' });
      if (revenueFiat >= 10000) badges.push({ id: 'whale', name: '$10K Volume', icon: '🐋', color: 'text-purple-400' });

      setStats({ sales, revenueFiat, btc, xmr, badges });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to fetch vendor stats', e);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [identity, targetPubkey]);

  return { stats, loading, fetchStats };
}
=========================================
FILE: src/hooks/useNIP44DM.js
=========================================

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';
import { saveMessage } from '../db';

export function useNIP44DM({ identity }) {
  const setDms = useChatStore((s) => s.setDms);
  const setTypingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.setTypingUsers);

  const sortMessages = (a, b) => {
    const tA = a.local_timestamp || (a.timestamp * 1000);
    const tB = b.local_timestamp || (b.timestamp * 1000);
    if (tA !== tB) return tA - tB;
    return (a.id || '').localeCompare(b.id || '');
  };

  // SEND A DM
  const sendDM = useCallback(async (recipientPubkey, payload, clientId = null, replyId = null) => {
    if (!recipientPubkey || !identity?.sk) return { success: false, error: 'Missing params' };

    // Defensive: trim and cap length
    let targetDm = recipientPubkey.trim();
    if (targetDm.length > 64) targetDm = targetDm.slice(0, 64);

    try {
      const { finalizeEvent, nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, targetDm);
      const encrypted = nip44.v2.encrypt(JSON.stringify(payload), shared);
      const tags = [['p', targetDm]];
      if (clientId) tags.push(['client', clientId]);
      if (replyId) tags.push(['e', replyId, '', 'reply']);
      const event = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 4,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags,
        content: encrypted
      }, identity.sk);
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, event));
      return { success: true, event, results };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[useNIP44DM] Send failed:', err);
      return { success: false, error: err.message };
    }
  }, [identity]);

  // SEND TYPING INDICATOR
  const sendTypingIndicator = useCallback(async (recipientPubkey) => {
    if (!recipientPubkey || !identity?.sk) return;
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent, nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, recipientPubkey);
      const encrypted = nip44.v2.encrypt(JSON.stringify({ type: 'typing' }), shared);
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 4,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['p', recipientPubkey]],
        content: encrypted
      }, identity.sk);
      pool.publish(generalRelays, ev);
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [identity]);

  // SUBSCRIBE TO INCOMING + OUTGOING DMs
  const subscribeToDMs = useCallback((since) => {
    let subs = [];
    const mounted = { current: true };

    const setup = async () => {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const dmFilters = [
        { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk], since },
        { kinds: [4], authors: [identity.pk], since }
      ];
      dmFilters.forEach(filter => {
        const sub = pool.subscribe(generalRelays, filter, {
          onevent: async (event) => {
            if (!mounted.current) return;
            try {
              const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
              const isSender = event.pubkey === identity.pk;
              const counterparty = isSender ? event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1] : event.pubkey;
              if (!counterparty) return;
              const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, counterparty);
              let text;
              try { text = nip44.v2.decrypt(event.content, shared); }
              catch (e) { return; }
              let data = {};
              try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { data = { type: 'text', message: text }; }

              if (data.type === 'typing') {
                const age = (Date.now() / 1000) - event.created_at;
                if (age < 10) setTypingUsers(prev => ({ ...prev, [counterparty]: Date.now() }));
                return;
              }
              if (data.type === 'gkey' || data.type === 'read' || data.type === 'pin') return;

              const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
              const clientId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];
              const newMsg = {
                id: event.id, clientId, isSender,
                timestamp: event.created_at, data,
                pubkey: event.pubkey,
                replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null
              };
              await saveMessage(counterparty, newMsg);
              setDms(prev => {
                const chatMsgs = prev[counterparty] || [];
                if (chatMsgs.find(m => m.id === event.id)) return prev;
                const filtered = chatMsgs.filter(m => m.clientId !== clientId);
                return { ...prev, [counterparty]: [...filtered, newMsg].sort(sortMessages) };
              });
            } catch (e) { console.error('Live DM error:', e); }
          }
        });
        subs.push(sub);
      });
    };
    setup();
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
      subs.forEach(s => { try { s.close(); } catch (e) {} });
    };
  }, [identity, setDms, setTypingUsers]);

  // FULL FETCH OF DM HISTORY
  const fetchDMs = useCallback(async () => {
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const [sentDms, recvDms] = await Promise.all([
      queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [4], authors: [identity.pk], limit: 50 }),
      queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk], limit: 50 })
    ]);
    const allDms = Array.from(new Map([...sentDms, ...recvDms].map(ev => [ev.id, ev])).values());
    for (const ev of allDms) {
      try {
        const isSender = ev.pubkey === identity.pk;
        const counterparty = isSender ? ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1] : ev.pubkey;
        if (!counterparty) continue;
        const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, counterparty);
        let text;
        try { text = nip44.v2.decrypt(ev.content, shared); }
        catch (e) { continue; }
        let data = {};
        try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { data = { type: 'text', message: text }; }
        if (data.type === 'gkey' || data.type === 'typing' || data.type === 'read' || data.type === 'pin') continue;
        const replyTag = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
        const clientId = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];
        await saveMessage(counterparty, {
          id: ev.id, clientId, isSender,
          timestamp: ev.created_at, data,
          pubkey: ev.pubkey,
          replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null,
          isGroup: false
        });
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    const counterparties = new Set();
    for (const ev of allDms) {
      const isSender = ev.pubkey === identity.pk;
      const counterparty = isSender ? ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1] : ev.pubkey;
      if (counterparty) counterparties.add(counterparty);
    }
    return [...counterparties];
  }, [identity]);

  return { sendDM, sendTypingIndicator, subscribeToDMs, fetchDMs };
}
