FILE: src/hooks/useReactions.js
=========================================

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, groupRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';

export function useReactions({ identity }) {
  const setReactions = useChatStore((s) => s.setReactions);

  const sendReaction = useCallback(async (msgId, emoji, isGroup, groupId, authorPubkey) => {
    console.log("🚨 SEND REACTION HOOK:", { msgId, emoji, isGroup, groupId, authorPubkey });
    if (!msgId || !emoji || !identity?.sk) return { success: false };
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      const tags = [['e', String(msgId)]];
      if (authorPubkey) tags.push(['p', String(authorPubkey)]);
      if (isGroup && groupId) tags.push(['h', String(groupId)]);

      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 7,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags,
        content: emoji
      }, identity.sk);

      const relays = isGroup ? groupRelays : generalRelays;
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(relays, ev));


      // Optimistic update
      setReactions((prev) => {
        const existing = prev[msgId] || [];
        const alreadyMine = existing.find((r) => r.pubkey === identity.pk && r.emoji === emoji);
        const filtered = existing.filter((r) => !(r.pubkey === identity.pk && r.emoji === emoji));
        if (alreadyMine) {
          return { ...prev, [msgId]: filtered };
        }
        return { ...prev, [msgId]: [...filtered, { emoji, pubkey: identity.pk }] };
      });

      return { success: true, results };
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[useReactions] send failed:', e);
      return { success: false, error: e.message };
    }
  }, [identity, setReactions]);

  const fetchReactions = useCallback(async (msgIds) => {
    if (!msgIds || msgIds.length === 0) return;
    try {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const allRelays = [...new Set([...generalRelays, ...groupRelays])];
      const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, allRelays, { kinds: [7], '#e': msgIds, limit: 500 }, 4000);

      const reactionMap = {};
      events.forEach((ev) => {
        const targetId = ev.tags.find((t) => t[0] === 'e')?.[1];
        if (!targetId) return;
        if (!reactionMap[targetId]) reactionMap[targetId] = [];
        if (!reactionMap[targetId].find((r) => r.pubkey === ev.pubkey && r.emoji === ev.content)) {
          reactionMap[targetId].push({ emoji: ev.content, pubkey: ev.pubkey });
        }
      });

      setReactions((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...reactionMap }));
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[useReactions] fetch failed:', e);
    }
  }, [identity, setReactions]);

  return { sendReaction, fetchReactions };
}

FILE: src/hooks/useGroupKeyReconciler.js
=========================================

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { getCurrentGroupKey, getAllGroupKeys, getGrantedEpochsFor, putGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyStore';
import { distributeGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyDistribution';
import { generateGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupCipher';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays } from '../lib/relays';
import { db, currentOwner } from '../db';
const lastRunByGroup = new Map();
export function useGroupKeyReconciler({ groupId, members, isOwner, identity }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!groupId || !identity?.sk || !identity?.pk) return;
    if (!Array.isArray(members)) return;
    if (members.length === 0 && !isOwner) return;
    if (!currentOwner()) return;
    const sig = members.slice().sort().join(',') + '|' + (isOwner ? '1' : '0');
    if (lastRunByGroup.get(groupId) === sig) return;
    lastRunByGroup.set(groupId, sig);
    reconcile({ groupId, members, isOwner, identity }).catch(err => { console.warn('[group-key] reconcile deferred', err); lastRunByGroup.delete(groupId); });
  }, [groupId, members, isOwner, identity?.pk, identity?.sk]);
}
async function reconcile({ groupId, members, isOwner, identity }) {
  const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
  const publish = async (ev) => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, ev));
    if (!results.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')) throw new Error('all relays rejected');
  };
  let current = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
  if (!current && isOwner) {
    const key = generateGroupKey();
    await putGroupKey(groupId, 1, key);
    current = { epoch: 1, bytes: key };
    await distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk, groupId, epoch: 1, keyBytes: key, recipients: dedupe([...members, identity.pk]), publish });
    return;
  }
  if (!current) return;
  if (isOwner) {
    const prev = await safeGetSnapshot(groupId);
    const prevSet = new Set(prev || []);
    const removed = [...prevSet].filter(pk => !members.includes(pk));
    if (removed.length > 0) {
      const nextEpoch = current.epoch + 1;
      const newKey = generateGroupKey();
      await putGroupKey(groupId, nextEpoch, newKey);
      const priorEpochs = (await getAllGroupKeys(groupId)).filter(e => e.epoch !== nextEpoch);
      await distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk, groupId, epoch: nextEpoch, keyBytes: newKey, recipients: dedupe([...members, identity.pk]), extraEpochs: priorEpochs, publish });
      await safePutSnapshot(groupId, members);
      return;
    }
    await safePutSnapshot(groupId, members);
  }
  const allEpochs = await getAllGroupKeys(groupId);
  const recipients = [];
  for (const pk of members) { const granted = await getGrantedEpochsFor(groupId, pk); if (!granted.has(current.epoch)) recipients.push(pk); }
  if (!recipients.length) return;
  await distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk, groupId, epoch: current.epoch, keyBytes: current.bytes, recipients: dedupe(recipients), extraEpochs: allEpochs.filter(e => e.epoch !== current.epoch), publish });
}
async function safeGetSnapshot(groupId) { try { const row = await db.groupMemberSigs.get(groupId); return row?.members || null; } catch { return null; } }
async function safePutSnapshot(groupId, members) {
  try { await db.groupMemberSigs.put({ groupId, signature: members.slice().sort().join(','), members: members.slice(), updatedAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) }); } catch {}
}
function dedupe(arr) { return Array.from(new Set(arr.filter(Boolean))); }

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { haptic } from './lib/haptics';
import { MessageSquare, Compass, User } from 'lucide-react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import { useIdentity } from './useIdentity'
import { useChatStore } from './store/chatStore'
import { usePanicWipe } from './hooks/usePanicWipe'
import { useGroupKeyMember } from './hooks/useGroupKeyMember'
import TutorialModal from './components/TutorialModal'
import LockScreen from './LockScreen'
import Shop from './Shop'
import Chat from './Chat'
import Wallet from './Wallet' 

class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component {
  constructor(props) { super(props); this.state = { hasError: false, error: null }; }
  static getDerivedStateFromError(error) { return { hasError: true, error }; }
  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-black text-red-500 p-6 font-mono flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">App Crashed</h1>
          <pre className="text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap bg-red-900/20 p-4 rounded-xl border border-red-500/50 w-full max-w-md overflow-auto">{this.state.error.toString()}</pre>
          <button onClick={() => window.location.reload()} className="mt-6 px-6 py-3 bg-white text-black font-bold rounded-xl">Reload</button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

function MainApp() {
  const identity = useIdentity()
  const panicWipeEnabled = useChatStore((s) => s.panicWipeEnabled);
  const theme = useChatStore((s) => s.theme);
  usePanicWipe(identity, panicWipeEnabled);
  useGroupKeyMember({ identity });
  if (identity?.pk) console.log('YOUR PUBKEY:', identity.pk)
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('chat')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (theme === 'dark') document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    else document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
  }, [theme]);


  useEffect(() => {
    if (identity?.pk && localStorage.getItem('cipher_new_account') === 'true') {
      setShowTutorial(true);
    }
  }, [identity?.pk]);

  const [showTutorial, setShowTutorial] = useState(false)
    

  if (identity.loadingSession) {
    return <div className="h-screen bg-black flex items-center justify-center"><div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-white/20 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin"></div></div>;
  }

  if (!identity.sk) return <LockScreen identity={identity} />

  return (
    <div className="h-full w-full bg-base text-white overflow-hidden flex justify-center">
      <TutorialModal isOpen={showTutorial} onClose={() => { setShowTutorial(false); localStorage.removeItem('cipher_new_account'); }} />
      <main className="w-full max-w-md h-full relative overflow-hidden">
        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          <motion.div
            key={activeTab}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: "easeOut" }}
            className="h-full flex flex-col overflow-hidden"
          >
            {activeTab === 'chat' && <Chat identity={identity} />}
            {activeTab === 'explore' && <Shop identity={identity} setActiveTab={setActiveTab} />}
            {activeTab === 'profile' && <Wallet identity={identity} />}
          </motion.div>
        </AnimatePresence>
      </main>

      <motion.nav 
        initial={{ y: 50 }} animate={{ y: 0 }} transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 300, damping: 30 }}
        className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-base/90 backdrop-blur-xl border-t border-white/10 pb-safe"
      >
        <div className="flex justify-around items-center h-16 max-w-md mx-auto px-6">
          <button onClick={() => { haptic(10); setActiveTab('chat'); }} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-16 h-full outline-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-0 transition-all active:scale-90 ${activeTab === 'chat' ? 'text-white' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}>
            <MessageSquare size={22} strokeWidth={activeTab === 'chat' ? 2.5 : 2} strokeLinecap="round" className={activeTab === 'chat' ? 'text-blue-500' : ''} />
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium">Chats</span>
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => { haptic(10); setActiveTab('explore'); }} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-16 h-full outline-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-0 transition-all active:scale-90 ${activeTab === 'explore' ? 'text-white' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}>
            <Compass size={22} strokeWidth={activeTab === 'explore' ? 2.5 : 2} className={activeTab === 'explore' ? 'text-blue-500' : ''} />
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium">Explore</span>
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => { haptic(10); setActiveTab('profile'); }} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-16 h-full outline-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-0 transition-all active:scale-90 ${activeTab === 'profile' ? 'text-white' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}>
            <User size={22} strokeWidth={activeTab === 'profile' ? 2.5 : 2} className={activeTab === 'profile' ? 'text-blue-500' : ''} />
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium">Profile</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </motion.nav>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function App() {
  return <ErrorBoundary><MainApp /></ErrorBoundary>
}

import { create } from 'zustand';

export const useChatStore = create((set, get) => ({
  // THEME
  theme: localStorage.getItem('cipher_theme') || 'dark',
  setTheme: (newTheme) => {
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_theme', newTheme);
    set({ theme: newTheme });
    if (newTheme === 'dark') document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    else document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
  },

  // NAVIGATION
  view: 'list',
  activeDm: null,
  activeGroup: null,
  setView: (view) => set({ view }),
  setActiveDm: (v) => set({ activeDm: v }),
  setActiveGroup: (v) => set({ activeGroup: v }),
  openChat: (type, id, groupObj = null) => {
    if (type === 'dm') {
      set({ view: 'dm', activeDm: id, activeGroup: null, typingUsers: {}, groupTypingUsers: {} });
    } else {
      set({ view: 'group', activeGroup: groupObj, activeDm: null, typingUsers: {}, groupTypingUsers: {} });
    }
  },
  closeChat: () => set({ view: 'list', activeDm: null, activeGroup: null }),

  // DATA
  dms: {},
  groups: [],
  groupMessages: {},
  profiles: {},
  groupMembers: [],
  pinnedMessages: {},
  unreadCounts: {},
  reactions: {},
  deletedMsgs: new Set(),

  setDms: (v) => set((s) => ({ dms: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.dms) : v })),
  setGroups: (v) => set((s) => ({ groups: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.groups) : v })),
  setGroupMessages: (v) => set((s) => ({ groupMessages: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.groupMessages) : v })),
  setProfiles: (v) => set((s) => ({ profiles: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.profiles) : v })),
  setGroupMembers: (v) => set({ groupMembers: v }),
  setPinnedMessages: (v) => set((s) => ({ pinnedMessages: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.pinnedMessages) : v })),
  setUnreadCounts: (v) => set((s) => ({ unreadCounts: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.unreadCounts) : v })),
  setReactions: (v) => set((s) => ({ reactions: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.reactions) : v })),
  setDeletedMsgs: (v) => set({ deletedMsgs: v }),

  // TYPING
  typingUsers: {},
  groupTypingUsers: {},
  setTypingUsers: (v) => set((s) => ({ typingUsers: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.typingUsers) : v })),
  setGroupTypingUsers: (v) => set((s) => ({ groupTypingUsers: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.groupTypingUsers) : v })),

  // SEARCH
  searchQuery: '',
  searchResults: [],
  isSearching: false,
  setSearchQuery: (v) => set({ searchQuery: v }),
  setSearchResults: (v) => set({ searchResults: v }),
  setIsSearching: (v) => set({ isSearching: v }),

  // UI MODALS
  showMenu: false,
  showCreateGroup: false,
  showGroupInfo: false,
  showMyProfile: false,
  showAddProduct: false,
  selectedMessage: null,
  profilePubkey: null,
  checkoutProduct: null,
  setShowMenu: (v) => set({ showMenu: v }),
  setShowCreateGroup: (v) => set({ showCreateGroup: v }),
  setShowGroupInfo: (v) => set({ showGroupInfo: v }),
  setShowMyProfile: (v) => set({ showMyProfile: v }),
  setShowAddProduct: (v) => set({ showAddProduct: v }),
  setSelectedMessage: (v) => set({ selectedMessage: v }),
  setProfilePubkey: (v) => set({ profilePubkey: v }),
  setCheckoutProduct: (v) => set({ checkoutProduct: v }),

  // INPUT
  newMessage: '',
  newGroupName: '',
  newGroupAbout: '',
  isChannel: false,
  replyingTo: null,
  setNewMessage: (v) => set({ newMessage: v }),
  setNewGroupName: (v) => set({ newGroupName: v }),
  setNewGroupAbout: (v) => set({ newGroupAbout: v }),
  setIsChannel: (v) => set({ isChannel: v }),
  setReplyingTo: (v) => set({ replyingTo: v }),

  // ROLE
  isVendor: localStorage.getItem('cipher_is_vendor') === 'true',
  setIsVendor: (v) => {
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_is_vendor', v.toString());
    set({ isVendor: v });
  },

  // PROFILE EDITOR
  myName: '',
  myUsername: '',
  myAbout: '',
  setMyName: (v) => set({ myName: v }),
  setMyUsername: (v) => set({ myUsername: v }),
  setMyAbout: (v) => set({ myAbout: v }),

  // PRODUCTS
  myProducts: (() => {
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cipher_my_products') || '[]'); }
    catch (e) { return []; }
  })(),
  newProduct: { name: '', priceUsd: '', image: '' },
  setMyProducts: (v) => {
    const products = typeof v === 'function' ? v(get().myProducts) : v;
    set({ myProducts: products });
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_my_products', JSON.stringify(products));
  },
  setNewProduct: (v) => set({ newProduct: v }),

  // LOADING
  panicWipeEnabled: localStorage.getItem('cipher_panic_wipe') === 'true',
  loading: true,
  uploadingImage: false,
  isRecording: false,
  setLoading: (v) => set({ loading: v }),
  setPanicWipeEnabled: (v) => {
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_panic_wipe', v.toString());
    set({ panicWipeEnabled: v });
  },
  setUploadingImage: (v) => set({ uploadingImage: v }),
  setIsRecording: (v) => set({ isRecording: v }),

  // TOAST
  toast: null,
  setToast: (toast) => {
    set({ toast });
    if (toast) setTimeout(() => set({ toast: null }), 3000);
  },
}));
